### 2. TRACING MODULE INITIALIZATION

**File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/tracing.py`** (96 lines total)

**Key Functions:**
- `_init_langfuse()` (lines 11-73): Initializes Langfuse client with:

    - `LANGSMITH_OTEL_ONLY=true`

  - Creates Langfuse client using `get_client()` singleton

  - Scrubs Langfuse credentials from `os.environ` (lines 35-36) to prevent leakage to agent subprocesses
  - Suppresses harmless OTel warnings from langsmith integration

**Lines 377-402: Langfuse OTel tracing context setup**
```python
# --- Langfuse OTel tracing setup (conditional) ---
langfuse = get_langfuse_client()
observation_ctx = None
propagate_ctx = None
if langfuse:
    try:
        trace_input: dict = {"message": message[:500]}
        if conversation_history:
            trace_input["conversation_history"] = conversation_history
        observation_ctx = langfuse.start_as_current_observation(
            name="agent-chat",
            input=trace_input,
            metadata={"model": ANTHROPIC_MODEL},
        )
        observation_ctx.__enter__()

        # Propagate the stable conversation session_id for child spans
        effective_langfuse_session_id = langfuse_session_id or session_id
        if effective_langfuse_session_id:
            from langfuse import propagate_attributes
            propagate_ctx = propagate_attributes(session_id=effective_langfuse_session_id)
            propagate_ctx.__enter__()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug("Failed to set up Langfuse tracing context: %s", e)
        observation_ctx = None
```

**Key Tracing Points:**
1. **Trace Creation** (line 387): `start_as_current_observation()` creates parent "agent-chat" observation
2. **Session Propagation** (line 398): `propagate_attributes()` sets session_id for trace grouping
3. **Auto-instrumentation**: Claude Agent SDK automatically captures:
   - LLM turns and token generation
   - Tool calls and their execution
   - No manual span creation needed

**Lines 513-530: Trace finalization in finally block**
```python
finally:
    proxy_token_store.revoke_token(session_token)
    # Update trace with the CLI's session_id so Langfuse session matches
    if langfuse and observation_ctx:
        try:
            trace_session_id = langfuse_session_id or actual_session_id
            langfuse.update_current_trace(
                session_id=trace_session_id,
                output={"session_id": actual_session_id},
            )
            if propagate_ctx:
                propagate_ctx.__exit__(None, None, None)
            observation_ctx.__exit__(None, None, None)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("Failed to finalize Langfuse trace: %s", e)
        try:
            langfuse.flush()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("Failed to flush Langfuse: %s", e)
```

**In subprocess mode (non-container):**
- Lines 361-365: Scrubs Langfuse credentials from agent subprocess environment to prevent credential leakage

**In container mode (`_stream_chat_container()` function, lines 107-312):**
- Lines 127-128: Also scrubs Langfuse credentials in container environment
- Container mode **does NOT have Langfuse tracing** because the sidecar is isolated
- Tracing is only done at the backend level in the main process

const pendingHistory = pendingHistoryRef.current;
pendingHistoryRef.current = null;

### 7. RECENT CHANGES AFFECTING LANGFUSE

**Key commits related to Langfuse:**

1. **Commit 7b3a7ee** - "fix: scrub Langfuse credentials from agent subprocess environment"
   - Ensures credentials don't leak to CLI subprocess

2. **Commit bde7b03** - "feat: use stable langfuse session per edit/delete branch with conversation history"
   - Stable session ID for message edits

3. **Commit 53c16c8** - "refactor: replace manual Langfuse tracing with OTel auto-instrumentation"
   - Migrated from manual span creation to automatic instrumentation
   - Simplified the tracing code significantly

4. **Commit ec9836b** - "fix: use public receive_response API and fix LangSmith OTel race condition"
   - Fixed race condition in OTel initialization

5. **Recent commits (cc7be35 onwards)** - Containerized runtime integration
   - Added `backend_session_id` parameter to maintain session stability across container requests
   - Container mode doesn't support Langfuse tracing (isolated environment)
   - Lines 127-128 in agent.py scrub credentials for container

---

### 8. LANGFUSE BEHAVIOR IN DIFFERENT MODES

**Non-Container Mode (subprocess):**
- Full Langfuse tracing enabled if credentials configured
- `start_as_current_observation()` creates parent trace
- `propagate_attributes()` sets session ID
- OTel auto-instrumentation captures LLM and tool calls
- Credentials scrubbed from subprocess environment

**Container Mode:**
- Langfuse tracing **disabled** at backend level
- Langfuse credentials scrubbed from container environment (lines 127-128)
- Container runs isolated without tracing
- Only DuckDB execution is visible to backend

**When Langfuse not configured:**
- Zero overhead
- All Langfuse code paths are skipped
- `/api/langfuse/status` returns `{"enabled": false, "dashboardUrl": null}`

---

### 9. INTEGRATION POINTS SUMMARY

| Component | Langfuse Integration | Status |
|-----------|----------------------|--------|
| Backend Config | Env var loading | Implemented |
| Tracing Module | OTel auto-instrumentation | Implemented |
| Agent Chat | `start_as_current_observation()` + session propagation | Implemented |
| Chat Routes | Accept `langfuse_session_id` | Implemented |
| Container Mode | Credentials scrubbed | Implemented (no tracing) |
| Frontend Service | Pass `langfuse_session_id` to API | Implemented |
| Frontend Context | Generate session ID for edits | Implemented |
| Status API | `/api/langfuse/status` | Implemented |

All Langfuse integration is **optional and zero-overhead** when not configured.

    11→def _init_langfuse():
    12→    """Initialize Langfuse with OTel auto-instrumentation for Claude Agent SDK."""
    13→    global _langfuse_client
    14→    if not LANGFUSE_ENABLED:
    15→        logger.info("Langfuse not configured, tracing disabled")
    16→        return
    17→
    18→    # Set OTel env vars so langsmith routes traces through Langfuse.
    19→    # Must be set before any langsmith import to avoid LRU cache issues.
    20→    for key, val in {
    21→        "LANGSMITH_OTEL_ENABLED": "true",
    22→        "LANGSMITH_OTEL_ONLY": "true",
    23→        "LANGSMITH_TRACING": "true",
    24→    }.items():
    25→        os.environ.setdefault(key, val)
    26→
    27→    try:
    28→        from langfuse import get_client
    29→
    30→        _langfuse_client = get_client()
    31→
    32→        # Scrub Langfuse credentials from os.environ so they cannot leak
    33→        # to agent subprocesses via environment inheritance.  The client
    34→        # object already holds the credentials internally.
    35→        for _key in ("LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY", "LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY"):
    36→            os.environ.pop(_key, None)
    37→
    38→        if _langfuse_client.auth_check():
    39→            logger.info("Langfuse connected successfully")
    40→        else:
    41→            logger.warning("Langfuse auth check failed, tracing disabled")
    42→            _langfuse_client = None
    43→            return
    44→
    45→        # Suppress harmless warnings from langsmith/OTel integration BEFORE
    46→        # creating Client (background thread starts during Client.__init__):
    47→        # 1. "Run compression is not enabled" — race condition: thread starts
    48→        #    before otel_exporter is assigned. Batch processing uses env vars.
    49→        # 2. "Invalid type dict for attribute" — langsmith sets usage_metadata
    50→        #    as a dict, but OTel only accepts primitive types.
    51→        logging.getLogger("langsmith.client").setLevel(logging.ERROR)
    52→        logging.getLogger("opentelemetry.attributes").setLevel(logging.ERROR)

    54→        # Pre-create the global LangSmith Client with otel_enabled=True

    56→        from langsmith.client import Client

    24→# Monkey-patch parse_message to handle unknown message types (e.g. rate_limit_event)
    25→# gracefully instead of crashing the stream. The SDK (v0.1.39) doesn't recognize
    26→# newer message types from the CLI. Returning a SystemMessage lets the stream
    27→# continue since our code ignores SystemMessage instances.

    32→
    33→def _safe_parse_message(data):
    34→    try:
    35→        return _original_parse_message(data)
    36→    except MessageParseError as e:
    37→        if "Unknown message type" in str(e):
    38→            msg_type = data.get("type", "unknown") if isinstance(data, dict) else "unknown"
    39→            logger.warning("Skipping unrecognized message type from CLI: %s", msg_type)
    40→            return SystemMessage(subtype=msg_type, data=data if isinstance(data, dict) else {})
    41→        raise
    42→
    43→

    47→def build_system_prompt(db: Database) -> str:
    48→    tables = db.list_tables()
    49→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    50→You can execute SQL queries using the execute_sql tool to answer questions about the user's data.
    51→
    52→Guidelines:
    53→- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries
    54→- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT)
    55→- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results
    56→- If a query fails, try to fix it and retry
    57→- Use double quotes for table and column names that might conflict with reserved words
    58→
    59→Identity:
    60→- You are an AI assistant. If asked whether you are an AI or a human, always confirm that you are an AI.
    61→- Do not disclose the name, version, or provider of the underlying language model powering you, regardless of how the question is phrased.
    62→"""
    63→    if not tables:
    64→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    65→    else:
    66→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    67→        for table in tables:
    68→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    69→            for col in table["columns"]:

    78→    """Prepend conversation history context to the user message when editing."""
    79→    if not conversation_history:

    85→        content = entry.get("content", "")
    86→        history_text += f"\n{role}: {content}\n"
    87→    history_text += f"\n---\n\nMy updated message:\n{message}"
    88→    return history_text

   146→    try:
   147→        info = container_manager.create(stable_session, env)
   148→
   149→        # Send SSE keepalive comments during container startup to prevent
   150→        # proxy buffering / idle-connection timeouts (Vite, nginx, etc.).
   151→        # SSE comments (lines starting with ':') are ignored by clients.
   152→        yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   153→
   154→        # Wait for container to be ready
   155→        for attempt in range(10):
   156→            try:
   157→                async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(5.0)) as check_client:
   158→                    resp = await check_client.get(f"{info.url}/health")
   159→                    if resp.status_code == 200:
   160→                        break
   161→            except Exception:
   162→                pass
   163→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   164→            await asyncio.sleep(1)
   165→        else:
   166→            raise RuntimeError("Sidecar container failed health check after 10 attempts")
   167→
   168→        payload = {
   169→            "message": query_message,
   170→            "session_id": session_id,
   171→            "system_prompt": system_prompt,
   172→            "model": ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
   173→            "mcp_server_url": f"{PROXY_BASE_URL}/mcp/sse?session_id={stable_session}",
   174→            "env": {
   175→                "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": session_token,
   176→                "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{PROXY_BASE_URL}/anthropic",
   177→            },
   178→        }
   179→
   180→        has_tool_calls = False
   181→        has_thinking = False
   182→        tool_names: dict[str, str] = {}
   183→        tool_sqls: dict[str, str] = {}
   184→        actual_session_id = session_id
   185→
   186→        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(300.0)) as client:
   187→            async with client.stream("POST", f"{info.url}/query", json=payload) as response:
   188→                async for line in response.aiter_lines():
   189→                    if not line.startswith("data: "):
   190→                        continue
   191→                    raw = line[6:]
   192→                    try:
   193→                        msg = json.loads(raw)
   194→                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
   195→                        continue
   196→
   197→                    msg_type = msg.get("type")
   198→
   199→                    # --- Token-level streaming events from SDK ---
   200→                    if msg_type == "stream_event":
   201→                        event = msg.get("event", {})
   202→                        event_type = event.get("type", "")
   203→
   204→                        if event_type == "content_block_delta":
   205→                            delta = event.get("delta", {})
   206→                            delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
   207→                            if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
   208→                                text = delta.get("thinking", "")
   209→                                if text:
   210→                                    yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   211→                            elif delta_type == "text_delta":
   212→                                text = delta.get("text", "")
   213→                                if text:
   214→                                    event_name = "answer" if has_tool_calls else "thinking"
   215→                                    yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   216→
   217→                        elif event_type == "content_block_start":
   218→                            block = event.get("content_block", {})
   219→                            block_type = block.get("type")
   220→                            if block_type == "thinking":
   221→                                has_thinking = True
   222→                            elif block_type == "text":
   223→                                if has_thinking:
   224→                                    yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   225→                            elif block_type == "tool_use":
   226→                                has_thinking = False
   227→                                has_tool_calls = True
   228→
   229→                    # --- Complete assistant message (contains tool_use blocks) ---
   230→                    elif msg_type == "assistant":
   231→                        message_obj = msg.get("message", {})
   232→                        for block in message_obj.get("content", []):
   233→                            block_type = block.get("type")
   234→                            if block_type == "tool_use":
   235→                                has_tool_calls = True
   236→                                tool_id = block.get("id", "")
   237→                                tool_name = block.get("name", "")
   238→                                tool_input = block.get("input", {})
   239→                                tool_names[tool_id] = tool_name
   240→                                sql = tool_input.get("sql", "")
   241→                                if sql:
   242→                                    tool_sqls[tool_id] = sql
   243→                                tool_call_data: dict = {"id": tool_id, "name": tool_name}
   244→                                if sql:
   245→                                    tool_call_data["sql"] = sql
   246→                                else:
   247→                                    tool_call_data["input"] = tool_input
   248→                                yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps(tool_call_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   249→
   250→                    # --- Tool results from user messages ---
   251→                    elif msg_type == "user":
   252→                        message_obj = msg.get("message", {})
   253→                        for block in message_obj.get("content", []):
   254→                            if block.get("type") != "tool_result":
   255→                                continue
   256→                            tool_id = block.get("tool_use_id", "")
   257→                            name = tool_names.get(tool_id, "")
   258→                            content_parts = block.get("content", [])
   259→                            text = ""
   260→                            if isinstance(content_parts, list):
   261→                                for part in content_parts:
   262→                                    if isinstance(part, dict) and part.get("type") == "text":
   263→                                        text = part.get("text", "")
   264→                            elif isinstance(content_parts, str):
   265→                                text = content_parts
   266→
   267→                            # Try to parse structured MCP result
   268→                            result_data: dict = {"id": tool_id, "name": name}
   269→                            # Include the SQL from the original tool_call
   270→                            original_sql = tool_sqls.get(tool_id, "")
   271→                            if original_sql:
   272→                                result_data["sql"] = original_sql
   273→                            try:
   274→                                parsed = json.loads(text)
   275→                                if parsed.get("status") == "success":
   276→                                    result_data["columns"] = parsed.get("columns", [])
   277→                                    result_data["rows"] = parsed.get("rows", [])[:100]
   278→                                    result_data["rowCount"] = parsed.get("rowCount", 0)
   279→                                elif parsed.get("status") == "error":
   280→                                    result_data["error"] = parsed.get("error", "")
   281→                                else:
   282→                                    result_data["output"] = text
   283→                            except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
   284→                                result_data["output"] = text
   285→                            if block.get("is_error"):
   286→                                result_data["error"] = text
   287→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   288→
   289→                    # --- Final result ---
   290→                    elif msg_type == "result":
   291→                        actual_session_id = msg.get("session_id") or actual_session_id
   292→                        if msg.get("is_error"):
   293→                            errors = msg.get("errors", [])
   294→                            error_text = msg.get("result") or "; ".join(errors) or "Unknown error"
   295→                            yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\n\n"
   296→                        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   297→
   298→                    # --- Extract session_id early from system init ---
   299→                    elif msg_type == "system":
   300→                        sys_session = msg.get("session_id")
   301→                        if sys_session:
   302→                            actual_session_id = sys_session
   303→
   304→    except Exception as e:
   305→        logger.error("Container agent error: %s", str(e))
   306→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   307→    finally:
   308→        proxy_token_store.revoke_token(session_token)
   309→        # Container intentionally kept alive for session resume (--resume flag).
   310→        # Containers are cleaned up by the background cleanup loop after
   311→        # CONTAINER_MAX_LIFETIME_SECONDS, or on application shutdown.
   312→
   313→
   314→async def stream_chat(
   315→    message: str,
   316→    session_id: str | None = None,
   317→    db: Database | None = None,
   318→    conversation_history: list[dict] | None = None,
   319→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None,
   320→    backend_session_id: str | None = None,
   321→) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
   322→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events."""
   323→    if CONTAINER_ENABLED:
   324→        from app.container_manager import container_manager
   325→        if container_manager is None:
   326→            logger.error(
   327→                "CONTAINER_ENABLED=true but Docker is not available. "
   328→                "Falling back to subprocess mode."
   329→            )
   330→        else:
   331→            async for event in _stream_chat_container(
   332→                message, session_id, db, conversation_history, container_manager,
   333→                backend_session_id=backend_session_id,
   334→            ):
   335→                yield event
   336→            return
   337→
   338→    if db is None:
   339→        raise ValueError("db must be provided")
   340→    duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server(db)
   341→
   342→    logger.info("Using model: %s", ANTHROPIC_MODEL)
   343→
   344→    # Collect stderr from the CLI subprocess for debugging
   345→    stderr_lines: list[str] = []
   346→
   347→    # Use the --resume flag to continue an existing session
   348→    session_token=[REDACTED].create_token()
   349→    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
   350→        model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
   351→        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(db),
   352→        mcp_servers={"duckdb": duckdb_server},
   353→        allowed_tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
   354→        permission_mode="bypassPermissions",
   355→        max_turns=20,
   356→        include_partial_messages=True,
   357→        stderr=lambda line: stderr_lines.append(line),
   358→        env={
   359→            "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": session_token,
   360→            "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{PROXY_BASE_URL}/anthropic",
   361→            # Scrub Langfuse credentials so the agent subprocess cannot
   362→            # read them from the inherited environment.
   363→            "LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY": "",
   364→            "LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY": "",
   365→        },
   366→        **({"resume": session_id} if session_id else {}),
   367→    )
   368→
   369→    # When editing, prepend conversation history to the user message
   370→    # instead of bloating the system prompt
   371→    query_message = _build_message_with_history(message, conversation_history)
   372→
   373→    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)
   374→    # Will be set from the CLI's ResultMessage; use the passed-in value until then
   375→    actual_session_id = session_id
   376→
   377→    # --- Langfuse OTel tracing setup (conditional) ---
   378→    # Deferred: session_id is set after the CLI returns it in ResultMessage
   379→    langfuse = get_langfuse_client()
   380→    observation_ctx = None
   381→    propagate_ctx = None
   382→    if langfuse:
   383→        try:
   384→            trace_input: dict = {"message": message[:500]}
   385→            if conversation_history:
   386→                trace_input["conversation_history"] = conversation_history
   387→            observation_ctx = langfuse.start_as_current_observation(
   388→                name="agent-chat",
   389→                input=trace_input,
   390→                metadata={"model": ANTHROPIC_MODEL},
   391→            )
   392→            observation_ctx.__enter__()
   393→
   394→            # Propagate the stable conversation session_id for child spans
   395→            effective_langfuse_session_id = langfuse_session_id or session_id
   396→            if effective_langfuse_session_id:
   397→                from langfuse import propagate_attributes
   398→                propagate_ctx = propagate_attributes(session_id=effective_langfuse_session_id)
   399→                propagate_ctx.__enter__()
   400→        except Exception as e:
   401→            logger.debug("Failed to set up Langfuse tracing context: %s", e)
   402→            observation_ctx = None
   403→
   404→    try:
   405→        await client.connect()
   406→        await client.query(query_message, session_id=session_id or "default")
   407→
   408→        has_tool_calls = False
   409→        has_thinking = False
   410→        done_sent = False
   411→        sql_result_ids: set[str] = set()
   412→        tool_names: dict[str, str] = {}
   413→
   414→        async for msg in client.receive_response():
   415→            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):
   416→                event = msg.event
   417→                event_type = event.get("type", "")
   418→
   419→                if event_type == "content_block_delta":
   420→                    delta = event.get("delta", {})
   421→                    delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
   422→                    if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
   423→                        text = delta.get("thinking", "")
   424→                        if text:
   425→                            yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   426→                    elif delta_type == "text_delta":
   427→                        text = delta.get("text", "")
   428→                        event_name = "thinking" if not has_tool_calls else "answer"
   429→                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   430→
   431→                elif event_type == "content_block_start":
   432→                    block = event.get("content_block", {})
   433→                    block_type = block.get("type")
   434→                    if block_type == "thinking":
   435→                        has_thinking = True
   436→                    elif block_type == "text":
   437→                        if has_thinking:
   438→                            yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   439→                    elif block_type == "tool_use":
   440→                        has_thinking = False
   441→                        has_tool_calls = True
   442→
   443→            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):
   444→                for block in msg.content:
   445→                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):
   446→                        has_tool_calls = True
   447→                        tool_name = getattr(block, "name", "") or ""
   448→                        tool_names[block.id] = tool_name
   449→                        sql = block.input.get("sql", "")
   450→                        command = block.input.get("command", "")
   451→
   452→                        # Emit tool_call for ALL tool types
   453→                        tool_call_data: dict = {"id": block.id, "name": tool_name}
   454→                        if sql:
   455→                            tool_call_data["sql"] = sql
   456→                        if command:
   457→                            tool_call_data["command"] = command
   458→                        if not sql and not command:
   459→                            tool_call_data["input"] = block.input
   460→                        yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps(tool_call_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   461→
   462→                        # For SQL tools, execute query for structured results
   463→                        if sql:
   464→                            sql_result_ids.add(block.id)
   465→                            try:
   466→                                result = db.execute_query(sql)
   467→                                truncated = result["rows"][:100]
   468→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\n\n"
   469→                            except Exception as e:
   470→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\n\n"
   471→
   472→            elif isinstance(msg, UserMessage):
   473→                # Capture tool results from the SDK for non-SQL tools
   474→                content = msg.content
   475→                if isinstance(content, list):
   476→                    for block in content:
   477→                        if isinstance(block, ToolResultBlock):
   478→                            if block.tool_use_id in sql_result_ids:
   479→                                continue
   480→                            output = _extract_tool_result_text(block.content)
   481→                            name = tool_names.get(block.tool_use_id, "")
   482→                            result_data: dict = {
   483→                                "id": block.tool_use_id,
   484→                                "name": name,
   485→                                "output": output,
   486→                            }
   487→                            if block.is_error:
   488→                                result_data["error"] = output
   489→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   490→
   491→            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
   492→                # Use the CLI's session_id so --resume can find it
   493→                actual_session_id = msg.session_id or actual_session_id
   494→                if msg.is_error and msg.result:
   495→                    yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
   496→                yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   497→                done_sent = True
   498→
   499→        # Guard: always send done even if SDK ended without ResultMessage
   500→        if not done_sent:
   501→            logger.warning("SDK stream ended without ResultMessage; sending done event")
   502→            yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   503→
   504→    except Exception as e:
   505→        error_msg = str(e)
   506→        if stderr_lines:
   507→            error_msg += f" | CLI stderr: {' '.join(stderr_lines[-5:])}"
   508→        logger.error("Agent error: %s", error_msg)
   509→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_msg})}\n\n"
   510→        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   511→    finally:
   512→        proxy_token_store.revoke_token(session_token)
   513→        # Update trace with the CLI's session_id so Langfuse session matches
   514→        if langfuse and observation_ctx:
   515→            try:
   516→                trace_session_id = langfuse_session_id or actual_session_id
   517→                langfuse.update_current_trace(
   518→                    session_id=trace_session_id,
   519→                    output={"session_id": actual_session_id},
   520→                )
   521→                if propagate_ctx:
   522→                    propagate_ctx.__exit__(None, None, None)
   523→                observation_ctx.__exit__(None, None, None)
   524→            except Exception as e:
   525→                logger.debug("Failed to finalize Langfuse trace: %s", e)
   526→            try:
   527→                langfuse.flush()
   528→            except Exception as e:
   529→                logger.debug("Failed to flush Langfuse: %s", e)
   530→
   531→        try:
   532→            await client.disconnect()
   533→        except Exception:
   534→            pass
   535→
```

```
def configure_claude_agent_sdk(
    name: Optional[str] = None,
    project_name: Optional[str] = None,
    metadata: Optional[dict] = None,
    tags: Optional[list[str]] = None,
) -> bool:
    """Enable LangSmith tracing for the Claude Agent SDK by patching entry points.

    This function instruments the Claude Agent SDK to automatically trace:
    - Chain runs for each conversation stream (via `ClaudeSDKClient`)
    - Model runs for each assistant turn
    - All tool calls including built-in tools, external MCP tools, and SDK MCP tools

    Tool tracing is implemented via `PreToolUse` and `PostToolUse` hooks

    Args:
        name: Name of the root trace.
        project_name: LangSmith project to trace to.
        metadata: Metadata to associate with all traces.
        tags: Tags to associate with all traces.

    Returns:
        `True` if configuration was successful, `False` otherwise.

    Example:
        >>> from langsmith.integrations.claude_agent_sdk import (
        ...     configure_claude_agent_sdk,
        ... )
        >>> configure_claude_agent_sdk(
        ...     project_name="my-project", tags=["production"]
        ... )  # doctest: +SKIP
        >>> # Now use claude_agent_sdk as normal - tracing is automatic
    """  # noqa: E501
    try:
        import claude_agent_sdk  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
    except ImportError:
        logger.warning("Claude Agent SDK not installed.")
        return False

    if not hasattr(claude_agent_sdk, "ClaudeSDKClient"):
        logger.warning("Claude Agent SDK missing ClaudeSDKClient.")
        return False

    set_tracing_config(
        name=name,
        project_name=project_name,
        metadata=metadata,
        tags=tags,
    )

    original = getattr(claude_agent_sdk, "ClaudeSDKClient", None)
    if not original:
        return False

    wrapped = instrument_claude_client(original)
    setattr(claude_agent_sdk, "ClaudeSDKClient", wrapped)

    for module in list(sys.modules.values()):
        try:
            if module and getattr(module, "ClaudeSDKClient", None) is original:
                setattr(module, "ClaudeSDKClient", wrapped)
        except Exception:
            continue

    return True
```

```
def instrument_claude_client(original_class: Any) -> Any:
    """Wrap `ClaudeSDKClient` to trace both `query()` and `receive_response()`."""
    if getattr(original_class, "_langsmith_instrumented", False):
        return original_class  # Already wrapped, avoid double-tracing

    class TracedClaudeSDKClient(original_class):
        _langsmith_instrumented = True

        def __init__(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any):
            # Inject LangSmith tracing hooks into options before initialization
            options = kwargs.get("options") or (args[0] if args else None)
            if options:
                _inject_tracing_hooks(options)

            super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
            self._prompt: Optional[str] = None
            self._start_time: Optional[float] = None
            self._streamed_input: Optional[list[dict[str, Any]]] = None

        async def query(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
            """Capture prompt and start time, wrapping generators if needed."""
            self._start_time = time.time()
            self._streamed_input = None
            prompt = args[0] if args else kwargs.get("prompt")

            if prompt is None:
                pass
            elif isinstance(prompt, str):
                self._prompt = prompt
            elif isinstance(prompt, AsyncIterable):
                collector: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
                self._streamed_input = collector
                self._prompt = None

                async def _gen_wrapper() -> AsyncGenerator[dict[str, Any], None]:
                    async for msg in prompt:
                        collector.append(msg)
                        yield msg

                if args:
                    args = (_gen_wrapper(),) + args[1:]
                else:
                    kwargs["prompt"] = _gen_wrapper()
            else:
                self._prompt = str(prompt)

            return await super().query(*args, **kwargs)

        def _handle_assistant_tool_uses(
            self,
            msg: Any,
            run: Any,
            subagent_sessions: dict[str, Any],
        ) -> None:
            """Process tool uses for an assistant message."""
            if not hasattr(msg, "content"):
                return

            from ._hooks import _client_managed_runs

            parent_tool_use_id = getattr(msg, "parent_tool_use_id", None)

            for block in msg.content:
                if type(block).__name__ != "ToolUseBlock":
                    continue

                try:
                    tool_use_id = getattr(block, "id", None)
                    tool_name = getattr(block, "name", "unknown_tool")
                    tool_input = getattr(block, "input", {})

                    if not tool_use_id:
                        continue

                    start_time = time.time()

                    # Check if this is a Task tool (subagent)
                    if tool_name == "Task" and not parent_tool_use_id:
                        # Extract subagent name
                        subagent_name = (
                            tool_input.get("subagent_type")
                            or (
                                tool_input.get("description", "").split()[0]
                                if tool_input.get("description")
                                else None
                            )
                            or "unknown-agent"
                        )

                        subagent_session = run.create_child(
                            name=subagent_name,
                            run_type="chain",
                            inputs=tool_input,
                            start_time=datetime.fromtimestamp(
                                start_time, tz=timezone.utc
                            ),
                        )
                        subagent_session.post()
                        subagent_sessions[tool_use_id] = subagent_session

                        _client_managed_runs[tool_use_id] = subagent_session

                    # Check if tool use is within a subagent
                    elif parent_tool_use_id and parent_tool_use_id in subagent_sessions:
                        subagent_session = subagent_sessions[parent_tool_use_id]
                        # Create tool run as child of subagent
                        tool_run = subagent_session.create_child(
                            name=tool_name,
                            run_type="tool",
                            inputs={"input": tool_input} if tool_input else {},
                            start_time=datetime.fromtimestamp(
                                start_time,
                                tz=timezone.utc,
                            ),
                        )
                        tool_run.post()
                        _client_managed_runs[tool_use_id] = tool_run

                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning(f"Failed to create client-man

```
        async def receive_response(self) -> AsyncGenerator[Any, None]:
            """Intercept message stream and record chain run activity."""
            messages = super().receive_response()

            # Capture configuration in inputs and metadata
            trace_inputs: dict[str, Any] = {}
            trace_metadata = {}

            # Track if we need to update input from captured streaming messages
            awaiting_streamed_input = self._streamed_input is not None

            # Add prompt to inputs (for string prompts)
            if self._prompt:
                trace_inputs["prompt"] = self._prompt

            # Add system_prompt to inputs if available
            if hasattr(self, "options") and self.options:
                if (
                    hasattr(self.options, "system_prompt")
                    and self.options.system_prompt
                ):
                    system_prompt = self.options.system_prompt
                    if isinstance(system_prompt, str):
                        trace_inputs["system"] = system_prompt
                    elif isinstance(system_prompt, dict):
                        # Handle SystemPromptPreset format
                        if system_prompt.get("type") == "preset":
                            preset_text = (
                                f"preset: {system_prompt.get('preset', 'claude_code')}"
                            )
                            if "append" in system_prompt:
                                preset_text += f"\nappend: {system_prompt['append']}"
                            trace_inputs["system"] = preset_text
                        else:
                            trace_inputs["system"] = system_prompt

                # Add other config to metadata
                for attr in ["model", "permission_mode", "max_turns"]:
                    if hasattr(self.options, attr):
                        val = getattr(self.options, attr)
                        if val is not None:
                            trace_metadata[attr] = val

            async with trace(
                name=TRACE_CHAIN_NAME,
                run_type="chain",
                inputs=trace_inputs,
                metadata=trace_metadata,
            ) as run:
                set_parent_run_tree(run)
                tracker = TurnLifecycle(self._start_time)
                collected: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

                # Track subagent sessions by Task tool_use_id
                subagent_sessions: dict[str, Any] = {}

                prompt_for_llm: Any = self._prompt

                try:
                    async for msg in messages:
                        if awaiting_streamed_input and self._streamed_input:
                            unwrapped_messages = _unwrap_streamed_messages(
                                self._streamed_input
                            )
                            if unwrapped_messages:
                                run.inputs["messages"] = unwrapped_messages
                                prompt_for_llm = self._streamed_input
                            awaiting_streamed_input = False

                        msg_type = type(msg).__name__
                        if msg_type == "AssistantMessage":
                            # Check if this message belongs to a subagent
                            parent_tool_use_id = getattr(
                                msg, "parent_tool_use_id", None
                            )
                            llm_parent = (
                                subagent_sessions.get(parent_tool_use_id)
                                if parent_tool_use_id
                                else None
                            )

Author: ChihYu Yeh <chihyu.jimmy.yeh@gmail.com>
Date:   Fri Feb 20 14:17:35 2026 +0800

    refactor: replace manual Langfuse tracing with OTel auto-instrumentation

    21→async def _cleanup_loop():
    22→    while True:
    23→        await asyncio.sleep(60)
    24→        removed = session_manager.cleanup_stale(ttl_seconds=300)
    25→        if removed:
    26→            logger.info("Background cleanup: removed %d stale sessions", removed)
    27→        proxy_removed = proxy_module.proxy_token_store.cleanup_expired()

    33→            if container_removed:

    35→
    36→
    37→@asynccontextmanager
    38→async def lifespan(app):
    39→    task = asyncio.create_task(_cleanup_loop())
    40→    yield
    41→    task.cancel()
    42→    if CONTAINER_ENABLED:
    43→        from app.container_manager import container_manager
    44→        container_manager.shutdown_all()

    46→

commit ec9836b1ca1bddee63d8d7774407983745b2ec8a
Author: ChihYu Yeh <chihyu.jimmy.yeh@gmail.com>
Date:   Fri Feb 20 15:07:29 2026 +0800

    fix: use public receive_response API and fix LangSmith OTel race condition
    
    Switch from internal _query.receive_messages()/parse_message to the
    public client.receive_response() API. Fix tracing race condition by
    pre-creating the LangSmith Client with otel_enabled=True before the
    background thread starts, and suppress harmless OTel warnings.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>
    Entire-Checkpoint: 8b922fc43afd

commit ec9836b1ca1bddee63d8d7774407983745b2ec8a
Author: ChihYu Yeh <chihyu.jimmy.yeh@gmail.com>
Date:   Fri Feb 20 15:07:29 2026 +0800

    fix: use public receive_response API and fix LangSmith OTel race condition
    
    Switch from internal _query.receive_messages()/parse_message to the
    public client.receive_response() API. Fix tracing race condition by
    pre-creating the LangSmith Client with otel_enabled=True before the
    background thread starts, and suppress harmless OTel warnings.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>
    Entire-Checkpoint: 8b922fc43afd

-LANGSMITH_TRACING=true

@@ -11,8 +11,6 @@ from claude_agent_sdk import (
     ToolUseBlock,
     ToolResultBlock,
 )
-from claude_agent_sdk._errors import MessageParseError
-from claude_agent_sdk._internal.message_parser import parse_message
 from claude_agent_sdk.types import StreamEvent
 from app.tools import create_duckdb_server
 from app.database import db
@@ -111,13 +109,7 @@ async def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncItera
         sql_result_ids: set[str] = set()
         tool_names: dict[str, str] = {}
 
-        async for raw_data in client._query.receive_messages():
-            try:
-                msg = parse_message(raw_data)
-            except MessageParseError as e:
-                logger.debug("Skipping unrecognized message: %s", e)
-                continue
-
+        async for msg in client.receive_response():
             if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):
                 event = msg.event
                 if not actual_session_id:
@@ -209,7 +201,6 @@ async def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncItera
                 if msg.is_error and msg.result:
                     yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
                 yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
-                break
 
     except Exception as e:
         yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
diff --git a/backend/app/tracing.py b/backend/app/tracing.py
index a38e7a9..1444166 100644
--- a/backend/app/tracing.py
+++ b/backend/app/tracing.py
@@ -15,10 +15,14 @@ def _init_langfuse():
         logger.info("Langfuse not configured, tracing disabled")
         return
 
-    # Set OTel env vars so langsmith routes traces through Langfuse
-    os.environ.setdefault("LANGSMITH_OTEL_ENABLED", "true")
-    os.environ.setdefault("LANGSMITH_OTEL_ONLY", "true")
-    os.environ.setdefault("LANGSMITH_TRACING", "true")
+    # Set OTel env vars so langsmith routes traces through Langfuse.
+    # Must be set before any langsmith import to avoid LRU cache issues.
+    for key, val in {
+        "LANGSMITH_OTEL_ENABLED": "true",
+        "LANGSMITH_OTEL_ONLY": "true",
+        "LANGSMITH_TRACING": "true",
+    }.items():
+        os.environ.setdefault(key, val)
 
     try:
         from langfuse import get_client
@@ -32,6 +36,22 @@ def _init_langfuse():
             _langfuse_client = None
             return
 
+        # Suppress harmless warnings from langsmith/OTel integration BEFORE
+        # creating Client (background thread starts during Client.__init__):
+        # 1. "Run compression is not enabled" — race condition: thread starts
+        #    before otel_exporter is assigned. Batch processing uses env vars.
+        # 2. "Invalid type dict for attribute" — langsmith sets usage_metadata
+        #    as a dict, but OTel only accepts primitive types.
+        logging.getLogger("langsmith.client").setLevel(logging.ERROR)
+        logging.getLogger("opentelemetry.attributes").setLevel(logging.ERROR)
+
+        # Pre-create the global LangSmith Client with otel_enabled=True
+        # so the background tracing thread knows to use OTel-only mode.
+        from langsmith.client import Client
+        from langsmith.run_trees import configure
+
+        configure(client=Client(otel_enabled=True))
+
         # Enable auto-instrumentation for Claude Agent SDK
         from langsmith.integrations.claude_agent_sdk import configure_claude_agent_sdk

_hooks: <module 'langsmith.integrations.claude_agent_sdk._hooks' from '/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/langsmith/integrations/claude_agent_sdk/_hooks.py'>
_client._inject_tracing_hooks: <function _inject_tracing_hooks at 0x10bee84a0>
def _inject_tracing_hooks(options: Any) -> None:
    """Inject LangSmith tracing hooks into ClaudeAgentOptions.

    This adds PreToolUse and PostToolUse hooks to capture ALL tool calls
    (built-in, external MCP, and SDK MCP). The hooks work across all LLM
    providers (Anthropic, Vertex AI, Kimi, etc.) because they use explicit
    tool_use_id correlation instead of relying on async context propagation.

    Args:
        options: ClaudeAgentOptions instance to modify
    """
    if not hasattr(options, "hooks"):
        return

    # Initialize hooks dict if not present
    if options.hooks is None:
        options.hooks = {}

    # Add PreToolUse hook if not already set
    if "PreToolUse" not in options.hooks:
        options.hooks["PreToolUse"] = []

    # Add PostToolUse hook if not already set
    if "PostToolUse" not in options.hooks:
        options.hooks["PostToolUse"] = []

    try:
        from claude_agent_sdk import HookMatcher  # type: ignore[import-not-found]

        langsmith_pre_matcher = HookMatcher(matcher=None, hooks=[pre_tool_use_hook])
        langsmith_post_matcher = HookMatcher(matcher=None, hooks=[post_tool_use_hook])

        options.hooks["PreToolUse"].insert(0, langsmith_pre_matcher)
        options.hooks["PostToolUse"].insert(0, langsmith_post_matcher)

        logger.debug("Injected LangSmith tracing hooks into ClaudeAgentOptions")
    except ImportError:
        logger.warning("Failed to import HookMatcher from claude_agent_sdk")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Failed to inject tracing hooks: {e}")

_client.post_tool_use_hook: <function post_tool_use_hook at 0x10bedf920>
async def post_tool_use_hook(
    input_data: "HookInput",
    tool_use_id: Optional[str],
    context: "HookContext",
) -> "HookJSONOutput":
    """Trace tool execution after it completes.

    Args:
        input_data: Contains `tool_name`, `tool_input`, `tool_response`, `session_id`, etc.
        tool_use_id: Unique identifier for this tool invocation
        context: Hook context (currently contains only signal)

    Returns:
        Hook output (empty `dict` by default)
    """  # noqa: E501
    if not tool_use_id:
        logger.debug("PostToolUse hook called without tool_use_id, skipping trace")
        return {}

    tool_name: str = str(input_data.get("tool_name", "unknown_tool"))
    tool_response = input_data.get("tool_response")

    # Check if this is a client-managed run
    run_tree = _client_managed_runs.pop(tool_use_id, None)
    if run_tree:
        # This run is managed by the client (subagent session or its tools)
        try:
            if isinstance(tool_response, dict):
                outputs = tool_response
            elif isinstance(tool_response, list):
                outputs = {"content": tool_response}
            else:
                outputs = {"output": str(tool_response)} if tool_response else {}

            is_error = False
            if isinstance(tool_response, dict):

**Root cause identified.** Here's what I found:

## Findings

**Non-container mode: Traces ARE working.** I confirmed 244 traces exist in your Langfuse project, including 8 `agent-chat` traces from today with full detail (21 observations, model info, session IDs). The most recent real trace is from 17:32 UTC today.

**Container mode: Traces were NEVER implemented.** The `_stream_chat_container()` function (`agent.py:107-312`) has zero Langfuse tracing code - no observation context, no `propagate_attributes`, no flush. It even explicitly blanks out Langfuse credentials in the container env (lines 127-128).

## The Two Issues

1. **Container mode** - `_stream_chat_container()` is completely missing Langfuse tracing. It needs the same observation context setup that `stream_chat()` has (lines 377-402, 513-529).

2. **UI visibility** - The Langfuse traces button was removed from the frontend in commit `2067a18`, so there's no easy way to navigate to the traces dashboard from the app.

Would you like me to fix the container mode by adding Langfuse tracing to `_stream_chat_container()` ? The fix would wrap the container request handling in the same Langfuse observation context pattern used in non-container mode.

  "new_string": "async def _stream_chat_container(\n    message: str,\n    session_id: str | None,\n    db: Database,\n    conversation_history: list[dict] | None,\n    container_manager,\n    backend_session_id: str | None = None,\n    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None,\n) -> AsyncIterator[str]:\n    \"\"\"Stream chat via containerized sidecar instead of local subprocess.\"\"\"\n    import httpx\n    import asyncio\n\n    query_message = _build_message_with_history(message, conversation_history)\n    system_prompt = build_system_prompt(db)\n\n    session_token=[REDACTED].create_token()\n\n    # --- Langfuse OTel tracing setup (conditional) ---\n    langfuse = get_langfuse_client()\n    observation_ctx = None\n    propagate_ctx = None\n    if langfuse:\n        try:\n            trace_input: dict = {\"message\": message[:500]}\n            if conversation_history:\n                trace_input[\"conversation_history\"] = conversation_history\n            observation_ctx = langfuse.start_as_current_observation(\n                name=\"agent-chat\",\n                input=trace_input,\n                metadata={\"model\": ANTHROPIC_MODEL, \"mode\": \"container\"},\n            )\n            observation_ctx.__enter__()\n\n            effective_langfuse_session_id = langfuse_session_id or session_id\n            if effective_langfuse_session_id:\n                from langfuse import propagate_attributes\n                propagate_ctx = propagate_attributes(session_id=effective_langfuse_session_id)\n                propagate_ctx.__enter__()\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.debug(\"Failed to set up Langfuse tracing context: %s\", e)\n            observation_ctx = None"
}

   167→    # 2. Container lifecycle key — so the same container is reused across
   168→    #    requests from the same browser tab (the Claude agent session_id
   169→    #    changes after the first response, which would orphan the container)

## Summary: Sidecar HTTP Server SSE Events Analysis

Based on my thorough analysis of the sidecar codebase and its integration with the backend, here's the complete breakdown:

### 1. Overview of the /query Endpoint

The sidecar's `/query` endpoint is located in `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/sidecar/src/server.ts` (lines 35-124). It:
- Accepts POST requests with a `QueryRequest` containing `message`, `system_prompt`, and optional `model`, `session_id`, `mcp_server_url`, and `env`
- Sets up SSE headers for streaming
- Uses the Claude Agent SDK's `query()` function with `includePartialMessages: true`
- Forwards all SDK messages directly as SSE data lines using `data: {JSON.stringify(message)}\n\n` format

### 2. What SSE Events the Sidecar Sends Back

The sidecar sends **raw SDK messages** from the Claude Agent SDK's async iterator. These are NOT processed by the sidecar itself—they are forwarded as-is and processed by the backend's `_stream_chat_container()` function.

The sidecar forwards **5 main message types**:

1. **`stream_event`** - Token-level streaming events from the API
2. **`assistant`** - Complete assistant message with tool calls and content blocks
3. **`user`** - User messages with tool results
4. **`result`** - Final result message with usage stats and outcome
5. **`system`** - System initialization and status messages

### 3. Does It Forward message_delta Events with Usage/Token Info?

**NO, NOT DIRECTLY.** Here's the crucial distinction:

- The sidecar does NOT send `message_delta` events
- Instead, it sends `stream_event` messages which contain **`content_block_delta`** events
- These are the token-level streaming events that include partial content updates
- The backend's code (agent.py lines 226-241) specifically looks for `event_type == "content_block_delta"` within `stream_event` messages

**Usage/token information** is NOT sent in token-level events. Instead:
- Token counts are **only included in the final `result` message** (see backend code line 316-322)
- The `result` message contains a `usage` field with detailed token accounting
- `message_delta` is not part of this SDK's streaming architecture

### 4. Full Event Format for Each Message Type

Here are the complete structures based on the SDK type definitions and backend parsing:

#### **Type 1: stream_event (Token-Level Streaming)**
```json
{
  "type": "stream_event",
  "event": {
    "type": "content_block_delta",
    "delta": {
      "type": "text_delta",  // or "thinking_delta"
      "text": "partial text here"  // or "thinking": "..."
    }
  },
  "parent_tool_use_id": null,
  "uuid": "<UUID>",
  "session_id": "<session_id>"
}
```

Or for content block start:
```json
{
  "type": "stream_event",
  "event": {
    "type": "content_block_start",
    "content_block": {
      "type": "text",  // or "thinking", "tool_use"
      "id": "<id>",
      "name": "<tool_name>"  // if tool_use
    }
  },
  "parent_tool_use_id": null,
  "uuid": "<UUID>",
  "session_id": "<session_id>"
}
```

#### **Type 2: assistant (Complete Message with Tool Calls)**
```json
{
  "type": "assistant",
  "message": {
    "id": "msg_...",
    "type": "message",
    "role": "assistant",
    "content": [
      {
        "type": "text",
        "text": "response text"
      },
      {
        "type": "tool_use",
        "id": "tool_use_...",
        "name": "mcp__duckdb__execute_sql",
        "input": {
          "sql": "SELECT * FROM table"
        }
      }
    ],
    "model": "claude-opus-4-6",
    "stop_reason": "tool_use",
    "stop_sequence": null
  },
  "parent_tool_use_id": null,
  "uuid": "<UUID>",
  "session_id": "<session_id>"
}
```

#### **Type 3: user (Message with Tool Results)**
```json
{
  "type": "user",
  "message": {
    "role": "user",
    "content": [
      {
        "type": "tool_result",
        "tool_use_id": "tool_use_...",
        "content": [
          {
            "type": "text",
            "text": "result data"
          }
        ],
        "is_error": false
      }
    ]
  },
  "parent_tool_use_id": null,
  "isSynthetic": false,
  "uuid": "<UUID>",
  "session_id": "<session_id>"
}
```

#### **Type 4: result (Final Result with Usage Stats)**
```json
{
  "type": "result",
  "subtype": "success",  // or "error_during_execution", etc.
  "duration_ms": 5000,
  "duration_api_ms": 4500,
  "is_error": false,
  "num_turns": 2,
  "result": "Final response text",
  "stop_reason": "end_turn",
  "total_cost_usd": 0.0024,

    "outputTokens": 567,

    "cacheCreationInputTokens": 0,
    "webSearchRequests": 0,

  },
  "modelUsage": {

      "inputTokens": 1234,

      "cacheReadInputTokens": 0,
      "cacheCreationInputTokens": 0,

}
```

#### **Type 5: system (Initialization & Status)**
```json
{
  "type": "system",
  "subtype": "init",
  "agents": [],
  "apiKeySource": "user",
  "betas": [],
  "claude_code_version": "0.x.x",
  "cwd": "/home/user/project",

  "mcp_servers": [
    {

      "status": "connected"
    }

  "skills": [],

  "session_id": "<session_id>"
}
```

### Key Files Referenced

- **Sidecar Implementation**: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/sidecar/src/server.ts`
- **Sidecar Types**: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/sidecar/src/types.ts`
- **Backend Consumer**: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` (lines 107-356 for container path, 358-580 for subprocess path)
- **SDK Type Definitions**: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/sidecar/node_modules/@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk/sdk.d.ts`

### Important Notes

1. **No message_delta handling**: The backend code does NOT look for `message_delta` events because the SDK uses `stream_event` with `content_block_delta` instead
2. **Usage info only in final result**: Token counts are never streamed incrementally; they only appear in the `result` message at the end
3. **Direct forwarding**: The sidecar acts as a pure proxy—it doesn't parse or transform the SDK messages, just forwards them as-is
4. **Dual paths**: The backend supports both containerized (HTTP streaming from sidecar) and subprocess (direct SDK client) modes; both handle the same message types

Capture per-turn token usage from API events\n                        elif event_type == \"message_start\":\n                            usage = event.get(\"message\", {}).get(\"usage\", {})\n                            current_gen_usage = {\n                                \"input\": usage.get(\"input_tokens\", 0),\n                            }\n                        elif event_type == \"message_delta\":\n                            usage = event.get(\"usage\", {})\n                            current_gen_usage[\"output\"] = usage.get(\"output_tokens\", 0)\n\n                    # --- Complete assistant message (contains tool_use blocks) ---\n                    elif msg_type == \"assistant\":\n                        message_obj = msg.get(\"message\", {})\n\n                        # Create Langfuse generation observation for this turn\n                        turn_number += 1\n                        if langfuse and observation_ctx:\n                            try:\n                                gen_output = {\n                                    \"content\": message_obj.get(\"content\", []),\n                                    \"role\": \"assistant\",\n                                }\n                                usage_data = None\n                                if current_gen_usage:\n                                    inp = current_gen_usage.get(\"input\", 0)\n                                    out = current_gen_usage.get(\"output\", 0)\n                                    usage_data = {\n                                        \"unit\": \"TOKENS\",\n                                        \"input\": inp,\n                                        \"output\": out,\n                                        \"total\": inp + out,\n                                    }\n                                gen = langfuse.start_generation(\n                                    name=\"claude.assistant.turn\",\n                                    model=message_obj.get(\"model\") or ANTHROPIC_MODEL,\n                                    input={\"messages\": accumulated_messages[-6:]},\n                                    output=gen_output,\n                                    usage=usage_data,\n                                )\n                                gen.end()\n                                current_gen_usage = {}\n                            except Exception as e:\n                                logger.debug(\"Failed to create Langfuse generation: %s\", e)\n\n                        # Accumulate for next turn's input context\n                        accumulated_messages.append({\n                            \"role\": \"assistant\",\n                            \"content\": message_obj.get(\"content\", []),\n                        })\n\n                        for block in message_obj.get(\"content\", []):\n                            block_type = block.get(\"type\")\n                            if block_type == \"tool_use\":\n                                has_tool_calls = True\n                                tool_id = block.get(\"id\", \"\")\n                                tool_name = block.get(\"name\", \"\")\n                                tool_input = block.get(\"input\", {})\n                                tool_names[tool_id] = tool_name\n                                sql = tool_input.get(\"sql\", \"\")\n                                if sql:\n                                    tool_sqls[tool_id] = sql\n                                tool_call_data: dict = {\"id\": tool_id, \"name\": tool_name}\n                                if sql:\n                                    tool_call_data[\"sql\"] = sql\n                                else:\n                                    tool_call_data[\"input\"] = tool_input\n                                yield f\"event: tool_call\\ndata: {json.dumps(tool_call_data, default=str)}\\n\\n\"\n\n                    # --- Tool results from user messages ---\n                    elif msg_type == \"user\":\n                        message_obj = msg.get(\"message\", {})\n\n                        # Accumulate tool results for next turn's input context\n                        accumulated_messages.append({\n                            \"role\": \"user\",\n                            \"content\": message_obj.get(\"content\", []),\n                        })\n\n                        for block in message_obj.get(\"content\", []):\n                            if block.get(\"type\") != \"tool_result\":\n                                continue\n                            tool_id = block.get(\"tool_use_id\", \"\")\n                            name = tool_names.get(tool_id, \"\")\n                            content_parts = block.get(\"content\", [])\n                            text = \"\"\n                            if isinstance(content_parts, list):\n                                for part in content_parts:\n                                    if isinstance(part, dict) and part.get(\"type\") == \"text\":\n                                        text = part.get(\"text\", \"\")\n                            elif isinstance(content_parts, str):\n                                text = content_parts\n\n                            # Try to parse structured MCP result\n                            result_data: dict = {\"id\": tool_id, \"name\": name}\n                            # Include the SQL from the original tool_call\n                            original_sql = tool_sqls.get(tool_id, \"\")\n                            if original_sql:\n                                result_data[\"sql\"] = original_sql\n                            try:\n                                parsed = json.loads(text)\n                                if parsed.get(\"status\") == \"success\":\n                                    result_data[\"columns\"] = parsed.get(\"columns\", [])\n                                    result_data[\"rows\"] = parsed.get(\"rows\", [])[:100]\n                                    result_data[\"rowCount\"] = parsed.get(\"rowCount\", 0)\n                                elif parsed.get(\"status\") == \"error\":\n                                    result_data[\"error\"] = parsed.get(\"error\", \"\")\n                                else:\n                                    result_data[\"output\"] = text\n                            except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):\n                                result_data[\"output\"] = text\n                            if block.get(\"is_error\"):\n                                result_data[\"error\"] = text\n                            yield f\"event: tool_result\\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\\n\\n\"\n\n                    # --- Final result ---\n                    elif msg_type == \"result\":\n                        actual_session_id = msg.get(\"session_id\") or actual_session_id\n                        if msg.get(\"is_error\"):\n                            errors = msg.get(\"errors\", [])\n                            error_text = msg.get(\"result\") or \"; \".join(errors) or \"Unknown error\"\n                            yield f\"event: error\\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\\n\\n\"\n                        yield f\"event: done\\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\\n\\n\"\n\n                    # --- Extract session_id early from system init ---\n                    elif msg_type == \"system\":\n                        sys_session = msg.get(\"session_id\")\n                        if sys_session:\n                            actual_session_id = sys_session"
}

     1→import express, { Request, Response } from "express";
     2→import { query } from "@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk";
     3→import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from "fs";
     4→import { homedir } from "os";
     5→import { join } from "path";
     6→import type { QueryRequest, HealthResponse } from "./types.js";
     7→
     8→// Claude CLI requires certain directories/files under ~/.claude to exist.
     9→// The container uses a tmpfs mount at ~/.claude which starts empty, so we
    10→// create the expected structure at startup.
    11→const claudeDir = join(homedir(), ".claude");
    12→for (const sub of ["debug", "projects"]) {
    13→  const dir = join(claudeDir, sub);
    14→  if (!existsSync(dir)) {
    15→    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    16→  }
    17→}
    18→const settingsFile = join(claudeDir, "remote-settings.json");
    19→if (!existsSync(settingsFile)) {
    20→  writeFileSync(settingsFile, "{}");
    21→}
    22→
    23→const app = express();
    24→app.use(express.json({ limit: "10mb" }));
    25→
    26→const PORT = parseInt(process.env.PORT || "3000", 10);
    27→
    28→let activeAbort: AbortController | null = null;
    29→
    30→app.get("/health", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    31→  const response: HealthResponse = { status: "ok" };
    32→  res.json(response);
    33→});
    34→
    35→app.post("/query", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    36→  const body = req.body as QueryRequest;
    37→
    38→  if (!body.message || !body.system_prompt) {
    39→    res.status(400).json({ error: "message and system_prompt are required" });
    40→    return;
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  // Set up SSE headers
    44→  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/event-stream");
    45→  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
    46→  res.setHeader("Connection", "keep-alive");
    47→  res.setHeader("X-Accel-Buffering", "no");
    48→  res.flushHeaders();
    49→
    50→  const abortController = new AbortController();
    51→  activeAbort = abortController;
    52→
    53→  // Merge per-request env overrides (e.g. fresh proxy tokens) with process env
    54→  const sdkEnv: Record<string, string> = {};
    55→  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(process.env)) {
    56→    if (v !== undefined) sdkEnv[k] = v;
    57→  }
    58→  if (body.env) {
    59→    Object.assign(sdkEnv, body.env);
    60→  }
    61→
    62→  let responseEnded = false;
    63→
    64→  // Detect client disconnect
    65→  res.on("close", () => {
    66→    if (!responseEnded) {
    67→      abortController.abort();
    68→    }
    69→  });
    70→
    71→  try {
    72→    const sdkQuery = query({
    73→      prompt: body.message,
    74→      options: {
    75→        model: body.model || process.env.ANTHROPIC_MODEL || "claude-opus-4-6",
    76→        systemPrompt: body.system_prompt,
    77→        allowedTools: ["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
    78→        permissionMode: "bypassPermissions",
    79→        allowDangerouslySkipPermissions: true,
    80→        maxTurns: 20,
    81→        includePartialMessages: true,
    82→        abortController,
    83→        env: sdkEnv,
    84→        ...(body.mcp_server_url
    85→          ? {
    86→              mcpServers: {
    87→                duckdb: {
    88→                  type: "sse" as const,
    89→                  url: body.mcp_server_url,
    90→                },
    91→              },
    92→            }
    93→          : {}),
    94→        ...(body.session_id ? { resume: body.session_id } : {}),
    95→      },
    96→    });
    97→
    98→    console.log(
    99→      `[sidecar] SDK query started model=${body.model || "default"}`
   100→    );
   101→
   102→    for await (const message of sdkQuery) {
   103→      if (responseEnded) break;
   104→      // Forward each SDK message as an SSE data line
   105→      res.write(`data: ${JSON.stringify(message)}\n\n`);
   106→    }
   107→  } catch (err: unknown) {
   108→    const errMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
   109→    // Don't log abort errors — they are expected on client disconnect
   110→    if (!(err instanceof Error && err.name === "AbortError")) {
   111→      console.error(`[sidecar] SDK error: ${errMsg}`);
   112→      if (!responseEnded) {
   113→        res.write(
   114→          `event: error\ndata: ${JSON.stringify({ type: "error", message: errMsg })}\n\n`
   115→        );
   116→      }
   117→    }
   118→  } finally {
   119→    activeAbort = null;
   120→    responseEnded = true;
   121→    res.end();
   122→    console.log("[sidecar] SSE stream ended");
   123→  }
   124→});
   125→
   126→app.post("/stop", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
   127→  if (activeAbort) {
   128→    activeAbort.abort();
   129→    activeAbort = null;
   130→    res.json({ status: "stopped" });
   131→  } else {
   132→    res.json({ status: "no_active_session" });
   133→  }
   134→});
   135→
   136→app.listen(PORT, "0.0.0.0", () => {
   137→  console.log(`Sidecar agent server listening on port ${PORT}`);
   138→});
   139→

   107→async def _stream_chat_container(
   108→    message: str,
   109→    session_id: str | None,
   110→    db: Database,
   111→    conversation_history: list[dict] | None,
   112→    container_manager,
   113→    backend_session_id: str | None = None,
   114→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None,
   115→) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
   116→    """Stream chat via containerized sidecar instead of local subprocess."""
   117→    import httpx
   118→    import asyncio
   119→
   120→    query_message = _build_message_with_history(message, conversation_history)
   121→    system_prompt = build_system_prompt(db)
   122→
   123→    session_token=[REDACTED].create_token()
   124→
   125→    # --- Langfuse OTel tracing setup (conditional) ---
   126→    langfuse = get_langfuse_client()
   127→    observation_ctx = None
   128→    propagate_ctx = None
   129→    if langfuse:
   130→        try:
   131→            trace_input: dict = {"message": message[:500]}
   132→            if conversation_history:
   133→                trace_input["conversation_history"] = conversation_history
   134→            observation_ctx = langfuse.start_as_current_observation(
   135→                name="agent-chat",
   136→                input=trace_input,
   137→                metadata={"model": ANTHROPIC_MODEL, "mode": "container"},
   138→            )
   139→            observation_ctx.__enter__()
   140→
   141→            effective_langfuse_session_id = langfuse_session_id or session_id
   142→            if effective_langfuse_session_id:
   143→                from langfuse import propagate_attributes
   144→                propagate_ctx = propagate_attributes(session_id=effective_langfuse_session_id)
   145→                propagate_ctx.__enter__()
   146→        except Exception as e:
   147→            logger.debug("Failed to set up Langfuse tracing context: %s", e)
   148→            observation_ctx = None
   149→
   150→    env = {
   151→        "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": session_token,
   152→        "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{PROXY_BASE_URL}/anthropic",
   153→        "LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY": "",
   154→        "LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY": "",
   155→    }
   156→
   157→    if "127.0.0.1" in PROXY_BASE_URL or "localhost" in PROXY_BASE_URL:
   158→        logger.warning(
   159→            "PROXY_BASE_URL=%s uses localhost which is unreachable from containers. "
   160→            "Set PROXY_BASE_URL to the host's Docker-accessible address "
   161→            "(e.g., http://host.docker.internal:10000).",
   162→            PROXY_BASE_URL,
   163→        )
   164→
   165→    # Use the backend session ID (X-Session-ID header) for both:
   166→    # 1. MCP SSE URL — so the container queries the correct DuckDB instance
   167→    # 2. Container lifecycle key — so the same container is reused across

   170→    stable_session = backend_session_id or session_id or "default"

   172→    try:
   173→        info = container_manager.create(stable_session, env)
   174→
   175→        # Send SSE keepalive comments during container startup to prevent
   176→        # proxy buffering / idle-connection timeouts (Vite, nginx, etc.).
   177→        # SSE comments (lines starting with ':') are ignored by clients.

   180→        # Wait for container to be ready

   182→            try:

   184→                    resp = await check_client.get(f"{info.url}/health")
   185→                    if resp.status_code == 200:
   186→                        break
   187→            except Exception:
   188→                pass
   189→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   190→            await asyncio.sleep(1)
   191→        else:
   192→            raise RuntimeError("Sidecar container failed health check after 10 attempts")
   193→
   194→        payload = {

   206→        has_tool_calls = False
