  "old_string": "async def _stream_chat_container(\n    message: str,\n    session_id: str | None,\n    db: Database,\n    conversation_history: list[dict] | None,\n    container_manager,\n    backend_session_id: str | None = None,\n    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None,\n) -> AsyncIterator[str]:\n    \"\"\"Stream chat via containerized sidecar instead of local subprocess.\"\"\"\n    import httpx\n    import asyncio\n\n    query_message = _build_message_with_history(message, conversation_history)\n    system_prompt = build_system_prompt(db)\n\n    session_token=[REDACTED].create_token()\n\n    # --- Langfuse OTel tracing setup (conditional) ---\n    langfuse = get_langfuse_client()\n    observation_ctx = None\n    propagate_ctx = None\n    if langfuse:\n        try:\n            trace_input: dict = {\"message\": message[:500]}\n            if conversation_history:\n                trace_input[\"conversation_history\"] = conversation_history\n            observation_ctx = langfuse.start_as_current_observation(\n                name=\"agent-chat\",\n                input=trace_input,\n                metadata={\"model\": ANTHROPIC_MODEL, \"mode\": \"container\"},\n            )\n            observation_ctx.__enter__()\n\n            effective_langfuse_session_id = langfuse_session_id or session_id\n            if effective_langfuse_session_id:\n                from langfuse import propagate_attributes\n                propagate_ctx = propagate_attributes(session_id=effective_langfuse_session_id)\n                propagate_ctx.__enter__()\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.debug(\"Failed to set up Langfuse tracing context: %s\", e)\n            observation_ctx = None\n\n    env = {\n        \"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\": session_token,\n        \"ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL\": f\"{PROXY_BASE_URL}/anthropic\",\n        \"LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY\": \"\",\n        \"LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY\": \"\",\n    }",

# Langfuse TypeScript/Node.js SDK for Traces and Generations

Based on the documentation provided, here's how to use the SDK:

## 1. Initialize the Client

```ts
import { LangfuseClient } from "@langfuse/client";

const langfuse = new LangfuseClient({
  publicKey: "your-public-key",
  secretKey: "your-secret-key",
  baseUrl: "https://cloud.langfuse.com",
});
```

The client "will automatically use the environment variables you set" if you configure `LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY`, `LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY`, and `LANGFUSE_BASE_URL`.

## 2. Create Traces with OpenTelemetry

The documentation shows that TypeScript tracing requires OpenTelemetry setup:

```ts
import { NodeSDK } from "@opentelemetry/sdk-node";
import { LangfuseSpanProcessor } from "@langfuse/otel";

const sdk = new NodeSDK({
  spanProcessors: [new LangfuseSpanProcessor()],
});
sdk.start();
```

Then use `startActiveObservation()` to create traced operations:

```ts
import { startActiveObservation } from "@langfuse/tracing";

await startActiveObservation("my-trace", async (span) => {
  span.update({
    input: "your input",
    output: "your output",
  });
});
```

## 3. Creating Generations

The document indicates you can "create [manual observations](/docs/observability/sdk/instrumentation#manual-observations)" for specialized LLM calls, though specific generation API details aren't provided in this excerpt.

## 4. Shutdown

For short-lived applications: "Shutdown flushes events and is required for short-lived applications"

```ts
await sdk.shutdown();
```

**Note:** Complete generation and trace attribute APIs require reviewing the [JS/TS API reference](https://js.reference.langfuse.com/).
```

I've performed the web search for "langfuse typescript SDK trace generation span API reference" and found comprehensive documentation. Here are the key findings:

## Overview

The Langfuse SDKs are built on top of OpenTelemetry. Langfuse maintains SDKs for Python and JavaScript/TypeScript.

## Trace & Observation Structure

Traces are the top-level entity in the Langfuse API and represent an execution flow in a LLM application usually triggered by an external event.

Spans represent durations of units of work in a trace. Generations are spans which are used to log generations of AI models. They contain additional attributes about the model, the prompt/completion.

## Creating Traces and Observations

You can create observations using trace.event({}), trace.span({}), and trace.generation({}).

## TypeScript SDK Setup

In your application, set the api keys to create a client using the Langfuse constructor with secretKey and publicKey.

In short-lived environments (e.g. serverless functions), make sure to always call langfuse.shutdownAsync() at the end to await all pending requests.

## Key Features

The Langfuse SDKs provides wrappers around OTel spans (LangfuseSpan, LangfuseGeneration) that offer convenient methods for interacting with Langfuse-specific features like scoring and media handling. You can also use these wrapper objects to add Langfuse trace attributes via update_trace() or use propagate_attributes() for automatic propagation to all child observations.

You can access the official API reference documentation directly on the Langfuse docs site at https://langfuse.com/docs/sdk/typescript for more detailed information about all available methods and parameters.

# Langfuse JS/TS SDK API Reference

Based on the provided documentation, here's what I can tell you about the API:

## 1. Creating a Langfuse Client

```typescript
import { LangfuseClient } from "npm:@langfuse/client";

const langfuse = new LangfuseClient();
// Uses environment variables: LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY, 
// LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY, LANGFUSE_BASE_URL
```

The document states the client "automatically uses the same environment variables we set earlier."

## 2-5. Trace, Generation, and Span Operations

Unfortunately, **the provided documentation does not include detailed API specifications** for:
- `langfuse.trace()` constructor parameters
- `trace.generation()` method signature
- Comprehensive parameter lists for model/input/output configuration
- Ending/completing semantics

The examples show these patterns:

```typescript
// Manual span creation approach
const span = startObservation('observation-name', {
    input: { /* input data */ },
});

const generation = span.startObservation('llm-call', {
    model: 'gpt-4',
    input: [{ role: 'user', content: '...' }],
}, { asType: "generation" });

generation.update({
    usageDetails: { input: 10, output: 5, total: 15 },
    output: { content: '...' }
}).end();

span.update({ output: '...' }).end();
```

## 6. Flushing

```typescript
await langfuseSpanProcessor.forceFlush();
```

**For complete API documentation**, the content recommends consulting the "[Langfuse JS/TS SDK Documentation](https://langfuse.com/docs/observability/sdk/typescript/overview)."

# TypeScript SDK Manual Tracing API

Based on the documentation provided, here's the complete API for manual observation creation:

## Core Methods

### `startObservation()`
Creates an observation without changing the active context.

```typescript
const span = startObservation(
  name: string,
  params?: {
    input?: any;
    output?: any;
    model?: string;
    metadata?: Record<string, any>;
    [key: string]: any;
  },
  options?: {
    asType?: "span" | "generation" | "tool";
    parentSpanContext?: {
      traceId: string;
      spanId: string;
      traceFlags: number;
    };
  }
);
```

## Creating Specific Observation Types

### Generations
```typescript
const generation = span.startObservation(
  "llm-call",
  {
    model: "gpt-4",
    input: [{ role: "user", content: "prompt" }],
    output: { content: "response" },
    usageDetails: { input: 10, output: 5 }
  },
  { asType: "generation" }
);
```

### Spans
```typescript
const childSpan = span.startObservation(
  "processing-step",
  { input: "data", output: "result" }
  // asType defaults to "span"
);
```

### Tools
```typescript
const toolCall = span.startObservation(
  "fetch-weather",
  { input: { city: "Paris" } },
  { asType: "tool" }
);
```

## Lifecycle Methods

### `.update()`
Updates observation data:
```typescript
span.update({
  input?: any;
  output?: any;
  metadata?: Record<string, any>;
  usageDetails?: { input: number; output: number };
  [key: string]: any;
});
```

### `.end()`
Closes the observation:
```typescript
span.end();
```

### Chaining
```typescript
generation
  .update({ output: { content: "Paris" } })
  .end();
```

## Trace-Level Operations

### `.updateTrace()`
Sets trace-level metadata independent of root observation:
```typescript
rootSpan.updateTrace({
  userId?: string;
  sessionId?: string;
  tags?: string[];
  metadata?: Record<string, any>;
  traceName?: string;
});
```

## Session and User Context

The documentation shows `sessionId` is set via `updateTrace()`:
```typescript
rootSpan.updateTrace({
  sessionId: "session-123",
  userId: "user-456"
});
```

Or via `propagateAttributes()`:
```typescript
await propagateAttributes(
  {
    userId: "user_123",
    sessionId: "session_abc"
  },
  async () => { /* nested traces */ }
);
```

## Nesting

Child observations automatically inherit parent context through the observation object:
```typescript
const parent = startObservation("parent");
const child = parent.startObservation("child");
child.end();
parent.end();
```

## Key Constraint

**Manual ending required**: "Failure to do so will result in incomplete or missing observations in Langfuse."
```

# Langfuse TypeScript SDK Packages and Setup

## NPM Packages

The recommended installation command for a complete TypeScript tracing setup is:

```bash
npm install @langfuse/tracing @langfuse/otel @opentelemetry/sdk-node
```

According to the documentation, these three packages work together: `@langfuse/tracing` provides "Core OpenTelemetry-based tracing functions," `@langfuse/otel` exports traces to Langfuse, and `@opentelemetry/sdk-node` is the OpenTelemetry foundation.

## Key Import Statements

For Node.js applications, the primary imports are:

```ts
import { NodeSDK } from "@opentelemetry/sdk-node";
import { LangfuseSpanProcessor } from "@langfuse/otel";
import { startActiveObservation } from "@langfuse/tracing";
import { LangfuseClient } from "@langfuse/client";
```

## Recommended Approach for Creating Traces

The documentation recommends using the "context manager" pattern. In Node.js, this means utilizing `startActiveObservation()` with async/await to create structured traces programmatically:

```ts
await startActiveObservation("my-trace-name", async (span) => {
  span.update({
    input: "your input",
    output: "your output",
  });
});
```

This approach automatically handles span hierarchy and context propagation without manual span management.

  "content": "import express, { Request, Response } from \"express\";\nimport { query } from \"@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk\";\nimport { Langfuse } from \"langfuse\";\nimport { mkdirSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from \"fs\";\nimport { homedir } from \"os\";\nimport { join } from \"path\";\nimport type { QueryRequest, HealthResponse } from \"./types.js\";\n\n// Claude CLI requires certain directories/files under ~/.claude to exist.\n// The container uses a tmpfs mount at ~/.claude which starts empty, so we\n// create the expected structure at startup.\nconst claudeDir = join(homedir(), \".claude\");\nfor (const sub of [\"debug\", \"projects\"]) {\n  const dir = join(claudeDir, sub);\n  if (!existsSync(dir)) {\n    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });\n  }\n}\nconst settingsFile = join(claudeDir, \"remote-settings.json\");\nif (!existsSync(settingsFile)) {\n  writeFileSync(settingsFile, \"{}\");\n}\n\n// Initialize Langfuse if credentials are available (reads from env vars\n// LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY, LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY, LANGFUSE_BASE_URL automatically)\nlet langfuse: Langfuse | null = null;\nif (process.env.LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY && process.env.LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY) {\n  langfuse = new Langfuse();\n  console.log(\"[sidecar] Langfuse tracing enabled\");\n}\n\nconst app = express();\napp.use(express.json({ limit: \"10mb\" }));\n\nconst PORT = parseInt(process.env.PORT || \"3000\", 10);\n\nlet activeAbort: AbortController | null = null;\n\napp.get(\"/health\", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  const response: HealthResponse = { status: \"ok\" };\n  res.json(response);\n});\n\napp.post(\"/query\", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  const body = req.body as QueryRequest;\n\n  if (!body.message || !body.system_prompt) {\n    res.status(400).json({ error: \"message and system_prompt are required\" });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Set up SSE headers\n  res.setHeader(\"Content-Type\", \"text/event-stream\");\n  res.setHeader(\"Cache-Control\", \"no-cache\");\n  res.setHeader(\"Connection\", \"keep-alive\");\n  res.setHeader(\"X-Accel-Buffering\", \"no\");\n  res.flushHeaders();\n\n  const abortController = new AbortController();\n  activeAbort = abortController;\n\n  // Merge per-request env overrides (e.g. fresh proxy tokens) with process env\n  const sdkEnv: Record<string, string> = {};\n  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(process.env)) {\n    if (v !== undefined) sdkEnv[k] = v;\n  }\n  if (body.env) {\n    Object.assign(sdkEnv, body.env);\n  }\n\n  let responseEnded = false;\n\n  // Detect client disconnect\n  res.on(\"close\", () => {\n    if (!responseEnded) {\n      abortController.abort();\n    }\n  });\n\n  // --- Langfuse tracing setup ---\n  const modelName = body.model || process.env.ANTHROPIC_MODEL || \"claude-opus-4-6\";\n  const trace = langfuse?.trace({\n    name: \"agent-chat\",\n    sessionId: body.langfuse_session_id || body.session_id || undefined,\n    input: { message: body.message.substring(0, 500) },\n    metadata: { model: modelName, mode: \"container\" },\n  });\n\n  // Per-turn usage tracking from stream events\n  let currentGenUsage: { input?: number; output?: number } = {};\n  // Accumulated messages for generation input context\n  const accumulatedMessages: Array<{ role: string; content: unknown }> = [\n    { role: \"user\", content: body.message },\n  ];\n\n  try {\n    const sdkQuery = query({\n      prompt: body.message,\n      options: {\n        model: modelName,\n        systemPrompt: body.system_prompt,\n        allowedTools: [\"mcp__duckdb__execute_sql\"],\n        permissionMode: \"bypassPermissions\",\n        allowDangerouslySkipPermissions: true,\n        maxTurns: 20,\n        includePartialMessages: true,\n        abortController,\n        env: sdkEnv,\n        ...(body.mcp_server_url\n          ? {\n              mcpServers: {\n                duckdb: {\n                  type: \"sse\" as const,\n                  url: body.mcp_server_url,\n                },\n              },\n            }\n          : {}),\n        ...(body.session_id ? { resume: body.session_id } : {}),\n      },\n    });\n\n    console.log(\n      `[sidecar] SDK query started model=${modelName}`\n    );\n\n    for await (const message of sdkQuery) {\n      if (responseEnded) break;\n      // Forward each SDK message as an SSE data line\n      res.write(`data: ${JSON.stringify(message)}\\n\\n`);\n\n      // --- Create Langfuse observations from SDK messages ---\n      if (!trace) continue;\n      const msg = message as Record<string, unknown>;\n\n      if (msg.type === \"stream_event\") {\n        // Capture per-turn token usage from API stream events\n        const event = (msg.event as Record<string, unknown>) || {};\n        const eventType = event.type as string;\n        if (eventType === \"message_start\") {\n          const msgData = (event.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};\n          const usage = (msgData.usage as Record<string, number>) || {};\n          currentGenUsage = { input: usage.input_tokens || 0 };\n        } else if (eventType === \"message_delta\") {\n          const usage = (event.usage as Record<string, number>) || {};\n          currentGenUsage.output = usage.output_tokens || 0;\n        }\n      } else if (msg.type === \"assistant\") {\n        // Create a generation observation for each assistant turn\n        const msgObj = (msg.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};\n        const content = msgObj.content as unknown[];\n\n        const usage =\n          currentGenUsage.input !== undefined\n            ? {\n                input: currentGenUsage.input || 0,\n                output: currentGenUsage.output || 0,\n                total:\n                  (currentGenUsage.input || 0) +\n                  (currentGenUsage.output || 0),\n                unit: \"TOKENS\" as const,\n              }\n            : undefined;\n\n        const gen = trace.generation({\n          name: \"claude.assistant.turn\",\n          model: (msgObj.model as string) || modelName,\n          input: { messages: accumulatedMessages.slice(-6) },\n          output: { content, role: \"assistant\" },\n          usage,\n        });\n        gen.end();\n        currentGenUsage = {};\n\n        // Accumulate for next turn's input context\n        accumulatedMessages.push({ role: \"assistant\", content });\n      } else if (msg.type === \"user\") {\n        // Accumulate tool results for next turn's input context\n        const msgObj = (msg.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};\n        accumulatedMessages.push({\n          role: \"user\",\n          content: msgObj.content,\n        });\n      } else if (msg.type === \"result\") {\n        // Finalize trace with output\n        const sessionId = msg.session_id as string | undefined;\n        trace.update({\n          output: { session_id: sessionId, completed: true },\n        });\n      }\n    }\n  } catch (err: unknown) {\n    const errMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n    // Don't log abort errors — they are expected on client disconnect\n    if (!(err instanceof Error && err.name === \"AbortError\")) {\n      console.error(`[sidecar] SDK error: ${errMsg}`);\n      if (!responseEnded) {\n        res.write(\n          `event: error\\ndata: ${JSON.stringify({ type: \"error\", message: errMsg })}\\n\\n`\n        );\n      }\n      if (trace) {\n        trace.update({ output: { error: errMsg } });\n      }\n    }\n  } finally {\n    activeAbort = null;\n    responseEnded = true;\n    res.end();\n    // Flush Langfuse events before the response is fully closed\n    if (langfuse) {\n      await langfuse.flushAsync().catch(() => {});\n    }\n    console.log(\"[sidecar] SSE stream ended\");\n  }\n});\n\napp.post(\"/stop\", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  if (activeAbort) {\n    activeAbort.abort();\n    activeAbort = null;\n    res.json({ status: \"stopped\" });\n  } else {\n    res.json({ status: \"no_active_session\" });\n  }\n});\n\napp.listen(PORT, \"0.0.0.0\", () => {\n  console.log(`Sidecar agent server listening on port ${PORT}`);\n});\n"
}

   144→            PROXY_BASE_URL,

   147→    # Use the backend session ID (X-Session-ID header) for both:

   149→    # 2. Container lifecycle key — so the same container is reused across

> AGENT

Done. Here's a summary of all changes:

## Changes Made

### Backend (`backend/app/agent.py`)
- **Import** `LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY`, `LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY`, `LANGFUSE_BASE_URL`, `LANGFUSE_ENABLED` from config
- **Pass real Langfuse credentials** to the container env (instead of blanking them) so the sidecar can trace directly
- **Pass `langfuse_session_id`** in the query payload to the sidecar
- **Removed** all Python-side Langfuse tracing from `_stream_chat_container()` (the sidecar handles it now)

### Sidecar (`sidecar/src/server.ts`)
- **Initialize Langfuse client** from env vars at startup
- **Create a trace** per `/query` request with session_id, input, and metadata
- **Create `claude.assistant.turn` GENERATION observations** for each assistant message with:
  - Model name
  - Input messages (accumulated conversation context, last 6 turns)
  - Output content (full assistant response)
  - Token usage (input/output from `message_start`/`message_delta` stream events)
- **Accumulate conversation messages** for generation input context
- **Update trace output** on completion or error
- **Flush Langfuse** asynchronously in the finally block

### Sidecar (`sidecar/src/types.ts`)
- Added `langfuse_session_id` to `QueryRequest`

### Sidecar (`sidecar/package.json`)
- Added `langfuse` dependency

**Note:** You'll need to rebuild the sidecar Docker image (`make sidecar-build`) for the changes to take effect in container mode.

> DEVELOPER

how to set LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY and LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY and LANGFUSE_BASE_URL in .env in sidecar so that it could read it the same as in backend

> AGENT

You don't need a separate `.env` in the sidecar. The credentials already flow automatically:

1. Backend reads `LANGFUSE_*` from `backend/.env` at startup (via `config.py`)
2. `_stream_chat_container()` passes them as Docker container env vars via `container_manager.create()`
3. The sidecar reads them from `process.env` at startup

So as long as your `backend/.env` has the keys set (which it does), the sidecar container will receive them automatically. Just rebuild the image:

```
make sidecar-build
```

Then run with `make dev-all` and the sidecar will log `[sidecar] Langfuse tracing enabled` on startup.

---

If you ever want to run the sidecar **outside Docker** for local dev (`npm run dev`), you'd either:
- Export the env vars in your shell, or
- Source the backend's `.env`:
  ```bash
  export $(grep LANGFUSE backend/.env | xargs) && npm run dev
  ```

The issue is that the sidecar sets `sessionId` at trace creation time, but for the first message both `langfuse_session_id` and `session_id` are `null` — the real session ID only arrives later in the `result` message. The backend handles this by calling `update_current_trace(session_id=...)` in the `finally` block after the stream completes.

Let me fix the sidecar to update the trace's `sessionId` when the actual session ID arrives.

> AGENT

Fixed. The problem was that the trace's `sessionId` field was never being set — we were only putting the session ID inside the `output` data object, not updating the trace attribute itself.

Now the sidecar:
1. Sets `sessionId` early from the `system` init message (so it appears in Langfuse sessions as soon as the stream starts)
2. Finalizes with the definitive `sessionId` from the `result` message (preferring `langfuse_session_id` from the frontend if provided, for edit/delete flows)

Rebuild with `make sidecar-build` to pick up the fix.

    28→# gracefully instead of crashing the stream. The SDK (v0.1.39) doesn't recognize

    33→_original_parse_message = _parser.parse_message

    35→
    36→def _safe_parse_message(data):
    37→    try:
    38→        return _original_parse_message(data)
    39→    except MessageParseError as e:
    40→        if "Unknown message type" in str(e):
    41→            msg_type = data.get("type", "unknown") if isinstance(data, dict) else "unknown"
    42→            logger.warning("Skipping unrecognized message type from CLI: %s", msg_type)
    43→            return SystemMessage(subtype=msg_type, data=data if isinstance(data, dict) else {})
    44→        raise

    50→def build_system_prompt(db: Database) -> str:
    51→    tables = db.list_tables()
    52→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    53→You can execute SQL queries using the execute_sql tool to answer questions about the user's data.
    54→
    55→Guidelines:
    56→- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries
    57→- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT)
    58→- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results
    59→- If a query fails, try to fix it and retry
    60→- Use double quotes for table and column names that might conflict with reserved words
    61→
    62→Identity:
    63→- You are an AI assistant. If asked whether you are an AI or a human, always confirm that you are an AI.
    64→- Do not disclose the name, version, or provider of the underlying language model powering you, regardless of how the question is phrased.
    65→"""
    66→    if not tables:

    68→    else:

   137→

   308→                    elif msg_type == "system":
   309→                        sys_session = msg.get("session_id")
   310→                        if sys_session:
   311→                            actual_session_id = sys_session
   312→
   313→    except Exception as e:
   314→        logger.error("Container agent error: %s", str(e))
   315→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   316→    finally:
   317→        proxy_token_store.revoke_token(session_token)
   318→        # Container intentionally kept alive for session resume (--resume flag).
   319→        # Containers are cleaned up by the background cleanup loop after
   320→        # CONTAINER_MAX_LIFETIME_SECONDS, or on application shutdown.
   321→
   322→
   323→async def stream_chat(
   324→    message: str,
   325→    session_id: str | None = None,
   326→    db: Database | None = None,
   327→    conversation_history: list[dict] | None = None,
   328→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None,
   329→    backend_session_id: str | None = None,
   330→) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
   331→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events."""
   332→    if CONTAINER_ENABLED:
   333→        from app.container_manager import container_manager
   334→        if container_manager is None:
   335→            logger.error(
   336→                "CONTAINER_ENABLED=true but Docker is not available. "
   337→                "Falling back to subprocess mode."
   338→            )
   339→        else:
   340→            async for event in _stream_chat_container(
   341→                message, session_id, db, conversation_history, container_manager,
   342→                backend_session_id=backend_session_id,
   343→                langfuse_session_id=langfuse_session_id,
   344→            ):
   345→                yield event
   346→            return
   347→
   348→    if db is None:
   349→        raise ValueError("db must be provided")
   350→    duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server(db)
   351→
   352→    logger.info("Using model: %s", ANTHROPIC_MODEL)
   353→
   354→    # Collect stderr from the CLI subprocess for debugging
   355→    stderr_lines: list[str] = []
   356→
   357→    # Use the --resume flag to continue an existing session
   358→    session_token=[REDACTED].create_token()
   359→    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
   360→        model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
   361→        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(db),
   362→        mcp_servers={"duckdb": duckdb_server},
   363→        allowed_tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
   364→        permission_mode="bypassPermissions",
   365→        max_turns=20,
   366→        include_partial_messages=True,
   367→        stderr=lambda line: stderr_lines.append(line),
   368→        env={
   369→            "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": session_token,
   370→            "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{PROXY_BASE_URL}/anthropic",
   371→            # Scrub Langfuse credentials so the agent subprocess cannot
   372→            # read them from the inherited environment.
   373→            "LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY": "",
   374→            "LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY": "",
   375→        },
   376→        **({"resume": session_id} if session_id else {}),
   377→    )
   378→
   379→    # When editing, prepend conversation history to the user message
   380→    # instead of bloating the system prompt
   381→    query_message = _build_message_with_history(message, conversation_history)
   382→
   383→    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)
   384→    # Will be set from the CLI's ResultMessage; use the passed-in value until then
   385→    actual_session_id = session_id
   386→
   387→    # --- Langfuse OTel tracing setup (conditional) ---
   388→    # Deferred: session_id is set after the CLI returns it in ResultMessage
   389→    langfuse = get_langfuse_client()
   390→    observation_ctx = None
   391→    propagate_ctx = None
   392→    if langfuse:
   393→        try:
   394→            trace_input: dict = {"message": message[:500]}
   395→            if conversation_history:
   396→                trace_input["conversation_history"] = conversation_history
   397→            observation_ctx = langfuse.start_as_current_observation(
   398→                name="agent-chat",
   399→                input=trace_input,
   400→                metadata={"model": ANTHROPIC_MODEL},
   401→            )
   402→            observation_ctx.__enter__()
   403→
   404→            # Propagate the stable conversation session_id for child spans
   405→            effective_langfuse_session_id = langfuse_session_id or session_id
   406→            if effective_langfuse_session_id:
   407→                from langfuse import propagate_attributes
   408→                propagate_ctx = propagate_attributes(session_id=effective_langfuse_session_id)
   409→                propagate_ctx.__enter__()
   410→        except Exception as e:
   411→            logger.debug("Failed to set up Langfuse tracing context: %s", e)
   412→            observation_ctx = None
   413→
   414→    try:
   415→        await client.connect()
   416→        await client.query(query_message, session_id=session_id or "default")
   417→
   418→        has_tool_calls = False
   419→        has_thinking = False
   420→        done_sent = False
   421→        sql_result_ids: set[str] = set()
   422→        tool_names: dict[str, str] = {}
   423→
   424→        async for msg in client.receive_response():
   425→            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):
   426→                event = msg.event
   427→                event_type = event.get("type", "")
   428→
   429→                if event_type == "content_block_delta":
   430→                    delta = event.get("delta", {})
   431→                    delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
   432→                    if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
   433→                        text = delta.get("thinking", "")
   434→                        if text:
   435→                            yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   436→                    elif delta_type == "text_delta":
   437→                        text = delta.get("text", "")
   438→                        event_name = "thinking" if not has_tool_calls else "answer"
   439→                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   440→
   441→                elif event_type == "content_block_start":
   442→                    block = event.get("content_block", {})
   443→                    block_type = block.get("type")
   444→                    if block_type == "thinking":
   445→                        has_thinking = True
   446→                    elif block_type == "text":
   447→                        if has_thinking:
   448→                            yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   449→                    elif block_type == "tool_use":
   450→                        has_thinking = False
   451→                        has_tool_calls = True
   452→
   453→            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):
   454→                for block in msg.content:
   455→                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):
   456→                        has_tool_calls = True
   457→                        tool_name = getattr(block, "name", "") or ""
   458→                        tool_names[block.id] = tool_name
   459→                        sql = block.input.get("sql", "")
   460→                        command = block.input.get("command", "")
   461→
   462→                        # Emit tool_call for ALL tool types
   463→                        tool_call_data: dict = {"id": block.id, "name": tool_name}
   464→                        if sql:
   465→                            tool_call_data["sql"] = sql
   466→                        if command:
   467→                            tool_call_data["command"] = command
   468→                        if not sql and not command:
   469→                            tool_call_data["input"] = block.input
   470→                        yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps(tool_call_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   471→
   472→                        # For SQL tools, execute query for structured results
   473→                        if sql:
   474→                            sql_result_ids.add(block.id)
   475→                            try:
   476→                                result = db.execute_query(sql)
   477→                                truncated = result["rows"][:100]
   478→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\n\n"
   479→                            except Exception as e:
   480→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\n\n"
   481→
   482→            elif isinstance(msg, UserMessage):
   483→                # Capture tool results from the SDK for non-SQL tools
   484→                content = msg.content
   485→                if isinstance(content, list):
   486→                    for block in content:
   487→                        if isinstance(block, ToolResultBlock):
   488→                            if block.tool_use_id in sql_result_ids:
   489→                                continue
   490→                            output = _extract_tool_result_text(block.content)
   491→                            name = tool_names.get(block.tool_use_id, "")
   492→                            result_data: dict = {
   493→                                "id": block.tool_use_id,
   494→                                "name": name,
   495→                                "output": output,
   496→                            }
   497→                            if block.is_error:
   498→                                result_data["error"] = output
   499→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   500→
   501→            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
   502→                # Use the CLI's session_id so --resume can find it
   503→                actual_session_id = msg.session_id or actual_session_id
   504→                if msg.is_error and msg.result:
   505→                    yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
   506→                yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   507→                done_sent = True
   508→
   509→        # Guard: always send done even if SDK ended without ResultMessage
   510→        if not done_sent:
   511→            logger.warning("SDK stream ended without ResultMessage; sending done event")
   512→            yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   513→
   514→    except Exception as e:
   515→        error_msg = str(e)
   516→        if stderr_lines:
   517→            error_msg += f" | CLI stderr: {' '.join(stderr_lines[-5:])}"
   518→        logger.error("Agent error: %s", error_msg)
   519→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_msg})}\n\n"
   520→        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   521→    finally:
   522→        proxy_token_store.revoke_token(session_token)
   523→        # Update trace with the CLI's session_id so Langfuse session matches
   524→        if langfuse and observation_ctx:
   525→            try:
   526→                trace_session_id = langfuse_session_id or actual_session_id
   527→                langfuse.update_current_trace(
   528→                    session_id=trace_session_id,
   529→                    output={"session_id": actual_session_id},
   530→                )
   531→                if propagate_ctx:
   532→                    propagate_ctx.__exit__(None, None, None)
   533→                observation_ctx.__exit__(None, None, None)
   534→            except Exception as e:
   535→                logger.debug("Failed to finalize Langfuse trace: %s", e)
   536→            try:
   537→                langfuse.flush()
   538→            except Exception as e:
   539→                logger.debug("Failed to flush Langfuse: %s", e)
   540→
   541→        try:
   542→            await client.disconnect()
   543→        except Exception:
   544→            pass
   545→

     1→import express, { Request, Response } from "express";
     2→import { query } from "@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk";
     3→import { Langfuse } from "langfuse";
     4→import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from "fs";
     5→import { homedir } from "os";
     6→import { join } from "path";
     7→import type { QueryRequest, HealthResponse } from "./types.js";
     8→
     9→// Claude CLI requires certain directories/files under ~/.claude to exist.
    10→// The container uses a tmpfs mount at ~/.claude which starts empty, so we
    11→// create the expected structure at startup.
    12→const claudeDir = join(homedir(), ".claude");
    13→for (const sub of ["debug", "projects"]) {
    14→  const dir = join(claudeDir, sub);
    15→  if (!existsSync(dir)) {
    16→    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    17→  }
    18→}
    19→const settingsFile = join(claudeDir, "remote-settings.json");
    20→if (!existsSync(settingsFile)) {
    21→  writeFileSync(settingsFile, "{}");
    22→}
    23→
    24→// Initialize Langfuse if credentials are available (reads from env vars
    25→// LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY, LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY, LANGFUSE_BASE_URL automatically)
    26→let langfuse: Langfuse | null = null;
    27→if (process.env.LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY && process.env.LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY) {
    28→  langfuse = new Langfuse();
    29→  console.log("[sidecar] Langfuse tracing enabled");
    30→}
    31→
    32→const app = express();
    33→app.use(express.json({ limit: "10mb" }));
    34→
    35→const PORT = parseInt(process.env.PORT || "3000", 10);
    36→
    37→let activeAbort: AbortController | null = null;
    38→
    39→app.get("/health", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    40→  const response: HealthResponse = { status: "ok" };
    41→  res.json(response);
    42→});
    43→
    44→app.post("/query", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    45→  const body = req.body as QueryRequest;
    46→
    47→  if (!body.message || !body.system_prompt) {
    48→    res.status(400).json({ error: "message and system_prompt are required" });
    49→    return;
    50→  }
    51→
    52→  // Set up SSE headers
    53→  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/event-stream");
    54→  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
    55→  res.setHeader("Connection", "keep-alive");
    56→  res.setHeader("X-Accel-Buffering", "no");
    57→  res.flushHeaders();
    58→
    59→  const abortController = new AbortController();
    60→  activeAbort = abortController;
    61→
    62→  // Merge per-request env overrides (e.g. fresh proxy tokens) with process env
    63→  const sdkEnv: Record<string, string> = {};
    64→  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(process.env)) {
    65→    if (v !== undefined) sdkEnv[k] = v;
    66→  }
    67→  if (body.env) {
    68→    Object.assign(sdkEnv, body.env);
    69→  }
    70→
    71→  let responseEnded = false;
    72→
    73→  // Detect client disconnect
    74→  res.on("close", () => {
    75→    if (!responseEnded) {
    76→      abortController.abort();
    77→    }
    78→  });
    79→
    80→  // --- Langfuse tracing setup ---
    81→  const modelName = body.model || process.env.ANTHROPIC_MODEL || "claude-opus-4-6";
    82→  const trace = langfuse?.trace({
    83→    name: "agent-chat",
    84→    sessionId: body.langfuse_session_id || body.session_id || undefined,
    85→    input: { message: body.message.substring(0, 500) },
    86→    metadata: { model: modelName, mode: "container" },
    87→  });
    88→
    89→  // Per-turn usage tracking from stream events
    90→  let currentGenUsage: { input?: number; output?: number } = {};
    91→  // Accumulated messages for generation input context
    92→  const accumulatedMessages: Array<{ role: string; content: unknown }> = [
    93→    { role: "user", content: body.message },
    94→  ];
    95→
    96→  try {
    97→    const sdkQuery = query({
    98→      prompt: body.message,
    99→      options: {
   100→        model: modelName,
   101→        systemPrompt: body.system_prompt,
   102→        allowedTools: ["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
   103→        permissionMode: "bypassPermissions",
   104→        allowDangerouslySkipPermissions: true,
   105→        maxTurns: 20,
   106→        includePartialMessages: true,
   107→        abortController,
   108→        env: sdkEnv,
   109→        ...(body.mcp_server_url
   110→          ? {
   111→              mcpServers: {
   112→                duckdb: {
   113→                  type: "sse" as const,
   114→                  url: body.mcp_server_url,
   115→                },
   116→              },
   117→            }
   118→          : {}),
   119→        ...(body.session_id ? { resume: body.session_id } : {}),
   120→      },
   121→    });
   122→
   123→    console.log(
   124→      `[sidecar] SDK query started model=${modelName}`
   125→    );
   126→
   127→    for await (const message of sdkQuery) {
   128→      if (responseEnded) break;
   129→      // Forward each SDK message as an SSE data line
   130→      res.write(`data: ${JSON.stringify(message)}\n\n`);
   131→
   132→      // --- Create Langfuse observations from SDK messages ---
   133→      if (!trace) continue;
   134→      const msg = message as Record<string, unknown>;
   135→
   136→      if (msg.type === "stream_event") {
   137→        // Capture per-turn token usage from API stream events
   138→        const event = (msg.event as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   139→        const eventType = event.type as string;
   140→        if (eventType === "message_start") {
   141→          const msgData = (event.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   142→          const usage = (msgData.usage as Record<string, number>) || {};
   143→          currentGenUsage = { input: usage.input_tokens || 0 };
   144→        } else if (eventType === "message_delta") {
   145→          const usage = (event.usage as Record<string, number>) || {};
   146→          currentGenUsage.output = usage.output_tokens || 0;
   147→        }
   148→      } else if (msg.type === "assistant") {
   149→        // Create a generation observation for each assistant turn
   150→        const msgObj = (msg.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   151→        const content = msgObj.content as unknown[];
   152→
   153→        const usage =
   154→          currentGenUsage.input !== undefined
   155→            ? {
   156→                input: currentGenUsage.input || 0,
   157→                output: currentGenUsage.output || 0,
   158→                total:
   159→                  (currentGenUsage.input || 0) +
   160→                  (currentGenUsage.output || 0),
   161→                unit: "TOKENS" as const,
   162→              }
   163→            : undefined;
   164→
   165→        const gen = trace.generation({
   166→          name: "claude.assistant.turn",
   167→          model: (msgObj.model as string) || modelName,
   168→          input: { messages: accumulatedMessages.slice(-6) },
   169→          output: { content, role: "assistant" },
   170→          usage,
   171→        });
   172→        gen.end();
   173→        currentGenUsage = {};
   174→
   175→        // Accumulate for next turn's input context
   176→        accumulatedMessages.push({ role: "assistant", content });
   177→      } else if (msg.type === "user") {
   178→        // Accumulate tool results for next turn's input context
   179→        const msgObj = (msg.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   180→        accumulatedMessages.push({
   181→          role: "user",
   182→          content: msgObj.content,
   183→        });
   184→      } else if (msg.type === "result") {
   185→        // Finalize trace: set the session_id so it appears in Langfuse sessions
   186→        const sessionId = msg.session_id as string | undefined;
   187→        const traceSessionId =
   188→          body.langfuse_session_id || sessionId || body.session_id;
   189→        trace.update({
   190→          ...(traceSessionId ? { sessionId: traceSessionId } : {}),
   191→          output: { session_id: sessionId, completed: true },
   192→        });
   193→      } else if (msg.type === "system") {
   194→        // Capture session_id early from system init for session grouping
   195→        const sessionId = msg.session_id as string | undefined;
   196→        if (sessionId && !body.langfuse_session_id) {
   197→          trace.update({ sessionId });
   198→        }
   199→      }
   200→    }
   201→  } catch (err: unknown) {
   202→    const errMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
   203→    // Don't log abort errors — they are expected on client disconnect
   204→    if (!(err instanceof Error && err.name === "AbortError")) {
   205→      console.error(`[sidecar] SDK error: ${errMsg}`);
   206→      if (!responseEnded) {
   207→        res.write(
   208→          `event: error\ndata: ${JSON.stringify({ type: "error", message: errMsg })}\n\n`
   209→        );
   210→      }
   211→      if (trace) {
   212→        trace.update({ output: { error: errMsg } });
   213→      }
   214→    }
   215→  } finally {
   216→    activeAbort = null;
   217→    responseEnded = true;
   218→    res.end();
   219→    // Flush Langfuse events before the response is fully closed
   220→    if (langfuse) {
   221→      await langfuse.flushAsync().catch(() => {});
   222→    }
   223→    console.log("[sidecar] SSE stream ended");
   224→  }
   225→});
   226→
   227→app.post("/stop", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
   228→  if (activeAbort) {
   229→    activeAbort.abort();
   230→    activeAbort = null;
   231→    res.json({ status: "stopped" });
   232→  } else {
   233→    res.json({ status: "no_active_session" });
   234→  }
   235→});
   236→
   237→app.listen(PORT, "0.0.0.0", () => {
   238→  console.log(`Sidecar agent server listening on port ${PORT}`);
   239→});
   240→

     1→from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header
     2→from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse
     3→from pydantic import BaseModel
     4→
     5→from app.agent import stream_chat
     6→from app.database import Database
     7→from app.dependencies import get_session_db
     8→
     9→router = APIRouter(prefix="/api", tags=["chat"])
    10→
    11→
    12→class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    13→    message: str
    14→    session_id: str | None = None
    15→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None
    16→    conversation_history: list[dict] = []
    17→
    18→
    19→class ChatEditRequest(BaseModel):
    20→    new_message: str
    21→    conversation_history: list[dict] = []
    22→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None
    23→
    24→
    25→@router.post("/chat")
    26→async def chat(
    27→    request: ChatRequest,
    28→    db: Database = Depends(get_session_db),
    29→    x_session_id: str = Header(...),
    30→):
    31→    return StreamingResponse(
    32→        stream_chat(
    33→            request.message,
    34→            request.session_id,
    35→            db,
    36→            conversation_history=request.conversation_history or None,
    37→            langfuse_session_id=request.langfuse_session_id,
    38→            backend_session_id=x_session_id,
    39→        ),
    40→        media_type="text/event-stream",
    41→        headers={
    42→            "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
    43→            "Connection": "keep-alive",
    44→            "X-Accel-Buffering": "no",
    45→        },
    46→    )
    47→
    48→
    49→@router.post("/chat/edit")
    50→async def chat_edit(
    51→    request: ChatEditRequest,
    52→    db: Database = Depends(get_session_db),
    53→    x_session_id: str = Header(...),
    54→):
    55→    """Edit a message: start a fresh session with conversation history as context."""
    56→    return StreamingResponse(
    57→        stream_chat(
    58→            request.new_message,

Perfect! Here is the full content of the `agentService.ts` file. Let me highlight the key parts you asked about:

## Key Findings

### `runAgentLoop` function (lines 96-133):
This function passes the parameters to `/api/chat` endpoint:
- **`langfuse_session_id`** - passed as `langfuse_session_id` in the request body (line 115)
- **`session_id`** - passed as `session_id` in the request body (line 114), using the `agentSessionId` parameter
- **`conversation_history`** - passed as `conversation_history` in the request body (line 116), defaulting to an empty array if null

The request body is (lines 112-117):
```json
{
  "message",
  "session_id": agentSessionId,
  "langfuse_session_id": langfuseSessionId,
  "conversation_history": conversationHistory ?? [],
}
```

Additionally, if a `userSessionId` is provided, it's sent as the `X-Session-ID` header (line 110).

### `runAgentEditLoop` function (lines 135-170):
This function passes parameters to `/api/chat/edit` endpoint:
- **`langfuse_session_id`** - passed as `langfuse_session_id` in the request body (line 153)
- **`conversation_history`** - passed as `conversation_history` in the request body (line 152)
- Note: This function does NOT pass `session_id` in the body (unlike `runAgentLoop`)

The request body is (lines 150-154):
```json
{
  "new_message": newMessage,
  "conversation_history": conversationHistory,
  "langfuse_session_id": langfuseSessionId,
}
```

Again, if a `userSessionId` is provided, it's sent as the `X-Session-ID` header (line 148).

> AGENT

Now I have a complete picture of the session ID flow. Let me compare the two implementations carefully.

**Backend non-container** (the reference behavior):
- Start: `propagate_attributes(session_id=langfuse_session_id or session_id)` 
- End: `update_current_trace(session_id=langfuse_session_id or actual_session_id)`
- Trace input includes `conversation_history` when available

**Sidecar** (current):
- Trace creation: `sessionId: body.langfuse_session_id || body.session_id || undefined`
- System handler: updates only if `!body.langfuse_session_id`
- Result handler: `body.langfuse_session_id || sessionId || body.session_id`
- Trace input doesn't include `conversation_history`

The session logic is actually logically equivalent, but there are a few differences that could cause confusing Langfuse dashboard behavior:

1. The sidecar trace `input` doesn't include `conversation_history`, making edit/delete traces harder to distinguish
2. The sidecar passes `body.message` which already has history prepended (by `_build_message_with_history`), so the trace input shows "Previous conversation..." instead of the original message

Let me fix this by passing the original message separately to the sidecar.

     1→import express, { Request, Response } from "express";
     2→import { query } from "@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk";
     3→import { Langfuse } from "langfuse";
     4→import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from "fs";
     5→import { homedir } from "os";
     6→import { join } from "path";
     7→import type { QueryRequest, HealthResponse } from "./types.js";
     8→
     9→// Claude CLI requires certain directories/files under ~/.claude to exist.
    10→// The container uses a tmpfs mount at ~/.claude which starts empty, so we
    11→// create the expected structure at startup.
    12→const claudeDir = join(homedir(), ".claude");
    13→for (const sub of ["debug", "projects"]) {
    14→  const dir = join(claudeDir, sub);
    15→  if (!existsSync(dir)) {
    16→    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    17→  }
    18→}
    19→const settingsFile = join(claudeDir, "remote-settings.json");
    20→if (!existsSync(settingsFile)) {
    21→  writeFileSync(settingsFile, "{}");
    22→}
    23→
    24→// Initialize Langfuse if credentials are available (reads from env vars
    25→// LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY, LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY, LANGFUSE_BASE_URL automatically)
    26→let langfuse: Langfuse | null = null;
    27→if (process.env.LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY && process.env.LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY) {
    28→  langfuse = new Langfuse();
    29→  console.log("[sidecar] Langfuse tracing enabled");
    30→}
    31→
    32→const app = express();
    33→app.use(express.json({ limit: "10mb" }));
    34→
    35→const PORT = parseInt(process.env.PORT || "3000", 10);
    36→
    37→let activeAbort: AbortController | null = null;
    38→
    39→app.get("/health", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    40→  const response: HealthResponse = { status: "ok" };
    41→  res.json(response);
    42→});
    43→
    44→app.post("/query", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    45→  const body = req.body as QueryRequest;
    46→
    47→  if (!body.message || !body.system_prompt) {
    48→    res.status(400).json({ error: "message and system_prompt are required" });
    49→    return;
    50→  }
    51→
    52→  // Set up SSE headers
    53→  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/event-stream");
    54→  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
    55→  res.setHeader("Connection", "keep-alive");
    56→  res.setHeader("X-Accel-Buffering", "no");
    57→  res.flushHeaders();
    58→
    59→  const abortController = new AbortController();
    60→  activeAbort = abortController;
    61→
    62→  // Merge per-request env overrides (e.g. fresh proxy tokens) with process env
    63→  const sdkEnv: Record<string, string> = {};
    64→  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(process.env)) {
    65→    if (v !== undefined) sdkEnv[k] = v;
    66→  }
    67→  if (body.env) {
    68→    Object.assign(sdkEnv, body.env);
    69→  }
    70→
    71→  let responseEnded = false;
    72→
    73→  // Detect client disconnect
    74→  res.on("close", () => {
    75→    if (!responseEnded) {
    76→      abortController.abort();
    77→    }
    78→  });
    79→
    80→  // --- Langfuse tracing setup ---
    81→  // Mirror the backend's session ID handling:
    82→  //   - langfuse_session_id (from frontend for edit/delete) takes priority
    83→  //   - session_id (CLI session from previous turn) as fallback
    84→  const modelName = body.model || process.env.ANTHROPIC_MODEL || "claude-opus-4-6";
    85→  const traceMessage = body.original_message || body.message;
    86→  const traceInput: Record<string, unknown> = {
    87→    message: traceMessage.substring(0, 500),
    88→  };
    89→  if (body.conversation_history && body.conversation_history.length > 0) {
    90→    traceInput.conversation_history = body.conversation_history;
    91→  }
    92→  const trace = langfuse?.trace({
    93→    name: "agent-chat",
    94→    sessionId: body.langfuse_session_id || body.session_id || undefined,
    95→    input: traceInput,
    96→    metadata: { model: modelName, mode: "container" },
    97→  });
    98→
    99→  // Per-turn usage tracking from stream events
   100→  let currentGenUsage: { input?: number; output?: number } = {};
   101→  // Accumulated messages for generation input context
   102→  const accumulatedMessages: Array<{ role: string; content: unknown }> = [
   103→    { role: "user", content: body.message },
   104→  ];
   105→
   106→  try {
   107→    const sdkQuery = query({
   108→      prompt: body.message,
   109→      options: {
   110→        model: modelName,
   111→        systemPrompt: body.system_prompt,
   112→        allowedTools: ["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
   113→        permissionMode: "bypassPermissions",
   114→        allowDangerouslySkipPermissions: true,
   115→        maxTurns: 20,
   116→        includePartialMessages: true,
   117→        abortController,
   118→        env: sdkEnv,
   119→        ...(body.mcp_server_url
   120→          ? {
   121→              mcpServers: {
   122→                duckdb: {
   123→                  type: "sse" as const,
   124→                  url: body.mcp_server_url,
   125→                },
   126→              },
   127→            }
   128→          : {}),
   129→        ...(body.session_id ? { resume: body.session_id } : {}),
   130→      },
   131→    });
   132→
   133→    console.log(
   134→      `[sidecar] SDK query started model=${modelName}`
   135→    );
   136→
   137→    for await (const message of sdkQuery) {
   138→      if (responseEnded) break;
   139→      // Forward each SDK message as an SSE data line
   140→      res.write(`data: ${JSON.stringify(message)}\n\n`);
   141→
   142→      // --- Create Langfuse observations from SDK messages ---
   143→      if (!trace) continue;
   144→      const msg = message as Record<string, unknown>;
   145→
   146→      if (msg.type === "stream_event") {
   147→        // Capture per-turn token usage from API stream events
   148→        const event = (msg.event as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   149→        const eventType = event.type as string;
   150→        if (eventType === "message_start") {
   151→          const msgData = (event.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   152→          const usage = (msgData.usage as Record<string, number>) || {};
   153→          currentGenUsage = { input: usage.input_tokens || 0 };
   154→        } else if (eventType === "message_delta") {
   155→          const usage = (event.usage as Record<string, number>) || {};
   156→          currentGenUsage.output = usage.output_tokens || 0;
   157→        }
   158→      } else if (msg.type === "assistant") {
   159→        // Create a generation observation for each assistant turn
   160→        const msgObj = (msg.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   161→        const content = msgObj.content as unknown[];
   162→
   163→        const usage =
   164→          currentGenUsage.input !== undefined
   165→            ? {
   166→                input: currentGenUsage.input || 0,
   167→                output: currentGenUsage.output || 0,
   168→                total:
   169→                  (currentGenUsage.input || 0) +
   170→                  (currentGenUsage.output || 0),
   171→                unit: "TOKENS" as const,
   172→              }
   173→            : undefined;
   174→
   175→        const gen = trace.generation({
   176→          name: "claude.assistant.turn",
   177→          model: (msgObj.model as string) || modelName,
   178→          input: { messages: accumulatedMessages.slice(-6) },
   179→          output: { content, role: "assistant" },
   180→          usage,
   181→        });
   182→        gen.end();
   183→        currentGenUsage = {};
   184→
   185→        // Accumulate for next turn's input context
   186→        accumulatedMessages.push({ role: "assistant", content });
   187→      } else if (msg.type === "user") {
   188→        // Accumulate tool results for next turn's input context
   189→        const msgObj = (msg.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   190→        accumulatedMessages.push({
   191→          role: "user",
   192→          content: msgObj.content,
   193→        });
   194→      } else if (msg.type === "result") {
   195→        // Finalize trace — mirrors backend's finally block:
   196→        //   trace_session_id = langfuse_session_id or actual_session_id
   197→        const actualSessionId = (msg.session_id as string | undefined) || body.session_id;
   198→        const traceSessionId = body.langfuse_session_id || actualSessionId;
   199→        trace.update({
   200→          ...(traceSessionId ? { sessionId: traceSessionId } : {}),
   201→          output: { session_id: actualSessionId },
   202→        });
   203→      } else if (msg.type === "system") {
   204→        // Capture session_id early — only when no langfuse_session_id override
   205→        // (matches backend's propagate_attributes(session_id=langfuse_session_id or session_id))
   206→        const sessionId = msg.session_id as string | undefined;
   207→        if (sessionId && !body.langfuse_session_id) {
   208→          trace.update({ sessionId });
   209→        }
   210→      }
   211→    }
   212→  } catch (err: unknown) {
   213→    const errMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
   214→    // Don't log abort errors — they are expected on client disconnect
   215→    if (!(err instanceof Error && err.name === "AbortError")) {
   216→      console.error(`[sidecar] SDK error: ${errMsg}`);
   217→      if (!responseEnded) {
   218→        res.write(
   219→          `event: error\ndata: ${JSON.stringify({ type: "error", message: errMsg })}\n\n`
   220→        );
   221→      }
   222→      if (trace) {
   223→        trace.update({ output: { error: errMsg } });
   224→      }
   225→    }
   226→  } finally {
   227→    activeAbort = null;
   228→    responseEnded = true;
   229→    res.end();
   230→    // Flush Langfuse events before the response is fully closed
   231→    if (langfuse) {
   232→      await langfuse.flushAsync().catch(() => {});
   233→    }
   234→    console.log("[sidecar] SSE stream ended");
   235→  }
   236→});
   237→
   238→app.post("/stop", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
   239→  if (activeAbort) {
   240→    activeAbort.abort();
   241→    activeAbort = null;
   242→    res.json({ status: "stopped" });
   243→  } else {
   244→    res.json({ status: "no_active_session" });
   245→  }
   246→});
   247→
   248→app.listen(PORT, "0.0.0.0", () => {
   249→  console.log(`Sidecar agent server listening on port ${PORT}`);
   250→});
   251→

Both files compile/parse successfully. Let me summarize the changes made and the session ID behavior analysis:

## Changes Made

### 1. `sidecar/src/types.ts` — Added fields for trace metadata
- Added `original_message` and `conversation_history` to `QueryRequest` interface

### 2. `backend/app/agent.py` — Pass original message and history to sidecar
- When `conversation_history` exists (edit/delete-resend), pass `original_message` and `conversation_history` as separate fields in the sidecar payload, so the sidecar's Langfuse trace shows the original user message (not the one with history prepended) and includes conversation history — matching the backend's trace input format

### 3. `sidecar/src/server.ts` — Improved Langfuse trace consistency
- **Trace input**: Uses `original_message` when available (matches backend's `message[:500]`), includes `conversation_history` (matches backend's `trace_input["conversation_history"]`)
- **Result handler**: Simplified to exactly mirror backend's `trace_session_id = langfuse_session_id or actual_session_id` pattern. Removed the extra `completed: true` field to match backend's `output: {"session_id": actual_session_id}`
- **System handler**: Already correct — only updates session when no `langfuse_session_id` override exists

## Session ID Flow Analysis

I verified the sidecar's session logic matches the backend across all scenarios:

| Scenario | `langfuse_session_id` | `session_id` | Trace Session |
|---|---|---|---|
| Normal first msg | null | null | CLI session (from `system` msg) |
| Normal follow-up | null | CLI session | CLI session |
| Edit | UUID | null | UUID (frontend) |
| Delete + resend | UUID | null | UUID (frontend) |

This matches the backend's pattern: `langfuse_session_id` always takes priority over the CLI's `actual_session_id`.

+      // --- Create Langfuse observations from SDK messages ---
+      if (!trace) continue;
+      const msg = message as Record<string, unknown>;
+
+      if (msg.type === "stream_event") {
+        // Capture per-turn token usage from API stream events
+        const event = (msg.event as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
+        const eventType = event.type as string;
+        if (eventType === "message_start") {
+          const msgData = (event.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
+          const usage = (msgData.usage as Record<string, number>) || {};
+          currentGenUsage = { input: usage.input_tokens || 0 };
+        } else if (eventType === "message_delta") {
+          const usage = (event.usage as Record<string, number>) || {};
+          currentGenUsage.output = usage.output_tokens || 0;
+        }
+      } else if (msg.type === "assistant") {
+        // Create a generation observation for each assistant turn
+        const msgObj = (msg.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
+        const content = msgObj.content as unknown[];
+
+        const usage =

+            ? {
+                input: currentGenUsage.input || 0,
+                output: currentGenUsage.output || 0,
+                total:

+                  (currentGenUsage.output || 0),
+                unit: "TOKENS" as const,
+              }
+            : undefined;
+
+        const gen = trace.generation({
+          name: "claude.assistant.turn",

+        // Accumulate for next turn's input context
+        accumulatedMessages.push({ role: "assistant", content });
+      } else if (msg.type === "user") {
